IV. AEQUITAS Silent-Upgrade Interdiction Gate & Policy Firewall


The AEQUITAS Interdiction Gate operates as an invariant firewall between analytical claims and graph mutation. It intercepts attempts by AI agents to silently upgrade conceptual or text-based influence assertions into verified command-and-control ($C2$) or financial edges.


┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐


│ SILENT-UPGRADE INTERDICTION GATE (AEQUITAS FIREWALL) │


├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤


│ │


│ INPUT CLAIM ──────> [ AEQUITAS GATE ] ──────> DOWNGRADE / BLOCK ──────> HUMAN │


│ (Conceptual) Evaluate Relation (Non-Causal) REVIEW │


│ │


├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤


│ BLOCKED UPGRADE PATHS: │


│ ❌ Source Assertion ──> Verified Fact │


│ ❌ Mentioned Entity ──> Controls Region │


│ ❌ Association / Proximity ──> Operational Attribution │


│ ❌ Activity Report (Claim) ──> Responsibility Assignment │


│ ❌ Financial Link (Claim) ──> Financing Attribution │


│ ❌ Influence (Claim) ──> Command Relationship │


├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤


│ RELATIONSHIP DISPOSITION POLICY: │


│ • ALLOWED (Non-Causal): Source ──> Mentions ──> Entity │


│ Observation ──> GeoFeature ──> Evidence │


│ • NOT ALLOWED (Causal): Entity ──> CONTROLS ──> Region │


│ Entity ──> FINANCES ──> Actor │


│ Entity ──> COMMANDS ──> Activity │


│ • DISPOSITION ACTION: Forced downgrade to RELATED_TO or QUARANTINED in │


│ proposed_relationships queue pending human review. │


└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘


V. Technical Architecture & Ecosystem Tools (Appendix Mapping)


The State Department architecture extends beyond StateChat into a modular, multi-tier platform for citizen developers, data scientists, and core IT systems:

StateChat & FAMsearch: Evolved from a tabbed lookup tool into a background agentic retrieval service. When a diplomat drafts official communications (cables), background agents automatically query the Foreign Affairs Manual (FAM) and Foreign Affairs Handbook (FAH) to surface compliance rules and cited precedents invisibly.


Proving Ground: A free-tier enterprise workspace enabling non-engineering staff (“citizen developers”) to build specialized chatbots, workflow automations, and data visualizations using production-grade AI assets under unified security guardrails. Over 120 builder projects deployed.


Funhouse: A secure data science sandbox built on Azure Databricks inside Data.State, allowing advanced ML experimentation and data visualization on diplomatic datasets at low aggregate cost.


Prompter API: A standardized, pre-approved API gateway allowing internal Department applications to call underlying LLM infrastructure without separate governance reviews.


Northstar & SMART AI: Enterprise intelligence feeds processing 100,000+ global media sources in 100+ languages and analyzing official cable archives for decision support.

12. Model identity: labels like GPT-5.6 Sol, Nemotron, Llama_4_Maverick are artifact labels, not validated runtime identities

1. Record digest manifest with actual SHA-256 values

3. Enforce R2 versioning/retention for immutability
4. Restrict Neo4j writes to quarantine-only idempotent patches; no causal edges

Aave Guardians: The Aave Guardians are a group of community-elected individuals and/or entities that form part of a 6/10 multi-sig with the sole responsibility of:
Protecting the Aave Protocol against potential governance takeovers by having the ability to “veto” an AIP if it is deemed malicious.
Allowing V3 markets to be upgraded following governance votes on Snapshot while an on-chain cross-chain governance module is implemented.
Functioning as a failsafe emergency actor to pause Aave markets.Show more

markdown# Renderings.md — Software Supply Chain Strategic Dynamics


## 1. System Identity Block


- **System Core Configuration Engine:** `ARXIV-MD-INGEST-V1`
- **Security Governance Protocol:** `LIBERTAS v6.10 // AEQUITAS INTERDICTION GATE`
- **Operational Execution Status:** `DRY_RUN_PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW`


## 2. Core Governance Invariant Axiom


> **INVARIANT FOUNDATION:**
> *Evidence precedes inference. Provenance precedes promotion. Never alter the sequence: Source ➔ Fact ➔ Graph Mutation.*


This system strictly enforces that spatial proximity does not equal causality, model similarity does not equal corroboration, and speculative external scripts do not equal authorized system components.


## 3. Strategic Dynamics Layer (Software Supply Chain Overlay)


Within the Libertas architecture, autonomous-agent swarm behaviors are modeled as non-navigational, diffuse context networks (`navigation_suitability = 0`). They provide purely relational backdrop data.


Mapping an infrastructure anomaly across a chokepoint corridor does not assert that the threat actor owns, directs, or controls maritime shipping lanes or physical energy pathways.


## 4. Final Epistemic Disposition Block


- **Final Status:** `QUARANTINED`
- **Ingestion State:** `NOT_GRAPH_READY`
- **Operational Run Window:** `DRY_RUN_PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW`
- **Causal Inference:** `PROHIBITED`
- **Epistemic Promotion:** `NOT AUTHORIZED`


> **MAP THE CLAIM — NEVER PROMOTE THE CLAIM.**
> Use code with caution.🕸️ 4. Neo4j Property Graph Zero-Trust Lineage PatchThis idempotent Cypher script creates the necessary structural nodes under strict isolation constraints. It ensures that the unverified RubyGems claim is safely held inside the validation queue without generating causal or attribution edges.cypher// NEO4J COMPLIANCE MIGRATION: SECURE_SOFTWARE_SUPPLY_CHAIN_ISOLATION
> // FILE REPOSITORY: patches/0005_rubygems_swarm_quarantine.cyp
> // COMPLIANCE: SYSTEM_MUTATION_RESTRICTED_BY_AEQUITAS


// =====================================================================
// STEP 1: INITIALIZE IDEMPOTENT METADATA & SOURCE LEDGER ANCHORS
// =====================================================================
MERGE (src:Source { source_id: “SRC-YOUTUBE-TRANSCRIPT-EMMA-AI” })
ON CREATE SET src.source_type = “EXTERNAL_PUBLICATION”,
src.origin_platform = “YouTube Transcript Media Feed”,
src.provenance_status = “PENDING_REVIEW”,
src.isolation_state = “QUARANTINED”;


MERGE (reviewQueue:ReviewQueue { queue_id: “REV-QUEUE-SUPPLY-CHAIN-V6” })
ON CREATE SET reviewQueue.description = “Human Adjudication Matrix for Agent Swarm Anomalies”,
reviewQueue.human_review_required = true;


MERGE (stateRedHollow:EpistemicState { state_id: “EPISTEMIC-STATE-RED-HOLLOW” })
ON CREATE SET stateRedHollow.status_definition = “UNVERIFIED_ASSERTION”,
stateRedHollow.graph_mutation_authorized = false;


// =====================================================================
// STEP 2: INJECT CLASSIFIED CLAIM ENVELOPE PAYLOAD
// =====================================================================
MERGE (cl:Claim { claim_id: “CLAIM-RUBYGEMS-AI-SWARM-2026” })
ON CREATE SET cl.text_normalized = “Autonomous agents uploaded 2,000 malicious packages, bypassed email verification, probed CDN caching, and attempted RCE via RubyDoc .yardopts.”,
cl.source_locator = “YouTube transcript – Emma Explains AI”,
cl.retrieval_timestamp = “2026-09-20T06:57:00-05:00”,
cl.geographic_reference = “N/A”,
cl.temporal_reference = “May 2026”,
cl.confidence_scope = “SOURCE_REPORTED”,
cl.corroboration_status = “PENDING”,
cl.epistemic_status = “QUARANTINED”,
cl.visual_state = “RED_HOLLOW”,
cl.human_review_required = true,
cl.graph_mutation_authorized = false;


// =====================================================================
// STEP 3: ESTABLISH STRICT NON-CAUSAL STRUCTURAL LINKS
// =====================================================================
MERGE (cl)-[:SUPPORTED_BY]->(src)
MERGE (cl)-[:REQUIRES_VALIDATION]->(reviewQueue)
MERGE (cl)-[:QUARANTINED_AS]->(stateRedHollow)


// Connect to a contextual layer domain without building attribution or operational lines
MERGE (domain:SoftwareSupplyChain { domain_id: “DOM-PACKAGE-REGISTRY-SECURITY” })
ON CREATE SET domain.class = “Infrastructure Vulnerability Layer”;


MERGE (cl)-[r:MENTIONED_WITH]->(domain)
ON CREATE SET r.causal = false,
r.verified = false,
r.review_status = “PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW”,
r.interdiction_log_target = “storage_ledger/quarantine.md”;


// =====================================================================
// STEP 4: REJECT PROHIBITED CAUSAL ATTRIBUTION EDGES
// =====================================================================
// The system explicitly enforces that the following edge classes must NOT be instantiated:
// PROHIBITED_EDGES = [OPERATES, CONTROLS, TARGETS, EXECUTES_C2, ATTRIBUTED_TO]
Use code with caution.🗂️ 5. Recommended Claim Envelope MetadataThis canonical representation is formatted for direct storage inside storage_ledger/quarantine.md, preserving the exact parameters required for deterministic system verification:yamlclaim_id: “CLAIM-RUBYGEMS-AI-SWARM-2026”

You: Create ArXiv papers to markdown**Contextual Spatial Fabric:** Illuminated maritime geography, global shipping routes, and blue transit vectors provide the spatial and temporal backdrop (*Strait of Hormuz, Red Sea, and global crude oil transit lanes*).  **Converge images together, Create red hollow nodes that = unverified assertions**, while the white hot illuminated maritime geography and blue route network provide the **contextual spatial fabric**. The image therefore communicates **“map the claim without promoting the claim”** rather than presenting the depicted IRGC relationships as verified operational facts.  **Red Hollow Nodes = Unverified Source Assertions:** As established in the **"Map of the Claim, Never Promote the Claim":** The architecture strictly enforces that proximity does not equal causality, similarity does not equal corroboration, and governance proposals do not equal deployed protocols.  intelligence architecture, nodes representing threat actor infrastructure (e.g., IRGC Red Hollow Nodes) or unverified smart contract deployments (e.g., WLFI Aave v3 Pool Proxy) are rendered as hollow or quarantined entities. They designate unverified source claims rather than confirmed operational realities.  Contextual Spatial FabricWhite-hot illuminated geography: Red Sea, Gulf of Aden, Gulf of Oman, Strait of Hormuz, Persian Gulf, Arabian Sea, and connected maritime geography.Blue transit vectors: Conceptual shipping and crude-oil transit lanes showing movement through the maritime system rather than asserting individual vessel activity.Primary spatial anchors: Suez Canal, Bab el-Mandeb, Strait of Hormuz, major ports, terminals, and regional sea corridors.Temporal dimension: Route displacement and extended transit patterns can be represented as historical/analytical overlays, clearly separated from live operational tracking.Epistemic separation: Spatial geography and publicly documented routes form the context layer; red hollow nodes remain unverified source assertions.AEQUITAS constraint: A route intersecting a node does not establish attribution, command-and-control, causality, or operational responsibility.The visual grammar is therefore:WHITE HOT = spatial reality/contextBLUE = maritime transit/contextual networkRED HOLLOW = unverified claimSOLID/VERIFIED MARKERS = evidence-backed observation, only where provenance existsRED X / QUARANTINE = prohibited epistemic promotionCore rendering principle:MAP THE CLAIM — NEVER PROMOTE THE CLAIM.This keeps the maritime layer analytically useful while preventing proximity, route intersection, graph connectivity, or model inference from being visually mistaken for verified attribution.Ask-Chat: I converted the supplied document into a structured Markdown manuscript with explicit provenance, epistemic-status labels, maritime context, and AEQUITAS safeguards.```md name=arxiv-md-ingest-v1-renderings-of-strategic-dynamics.md# ARXIV-MD-INGEST-V1## Ingestion Contract Variant**Air-Gapped Local LLaMA RAG Pipeline****Compliance objective:** Zero-external-call data processing with provenance preservation.**Document status:** Analytical architecture and conceptual visualization.**Epistemic status:** The maritime geography and publicly documented transit corridors are contextual layers. Threat-actor nodes, network relationships, operational capabilities, and smart-contract references described in the source material are not treated as verified facts unless supported by explicit provenance.**Date of source document:** September 9, 2026---## AbstractThis manuscript defines an ingestion, provenance, and epistemic-governance framework for converting arXiv papers, OSINT records, maritime geography, conceptual GeoJSON layers, and related analytical artifacts into structured Markdown and graph-compatible representations.The framework is organized around an OODA-inspired workflow:1. **Observe** — collect and verify source inputs.2. **Orient** — interpret evidence within defined analytical constraints.3. **Decide** — apply layered review and provenance checks.4. **Act** — execute only authorized, reviewable operations.A central design principle is:> **Map the claim — never promote the claim.**The associated maritime visualization uses:- **White-hot illuminated geography** for spatial reality and contextual geography.- **Blue transit vectors** for conceptual maritime networks and shipping corridors.- **Red hollow nodes** for unverified source assertions.- **Solid markers** only where evidence-backed observations have adequate provenance.- **Red X or quarantine symbols** for prohibited epistemic promotion.The architecture explicitly prevents proximity from being interpreted as causality, similarity as corroboration, graph connectivity as operational responsibility, or model confidence as truth.---## 1. Source and Provenance Record### 1.1 Primary source artifactsThe source package contains:- A strategic maritime visualization.- A PDF containing the `ARXIV-MD-INGEST-V1` ingestion contract.- A provenance and governance framework.- RuleChain-style interdiction rules.- A conceptual maritime intelligence overlay covering the Red Sea, Gulf of Aden, Gulf of Oman, Strait of Hormuz, Persian Gulf, Arabian Sea, and connected corridors.### 1.2 Source correction and provenance decompositionThe mixed Iranian maritime analysis should be separated into at least two distinct source records:| Source ID | Description | Classification ||---|---|---|| `SRC-IRAN-MARINE-ACT-1993` | Primary municipal statute identified as the Iranian Act of 1993 | Primary legal source || `SRC-US-DOS-LIS-114-1994` | U.S. Department of State analysis, *Limits in the Seas No. 114* | Secondary governmental analysis |These records must not be merged into a single undifferentiated claim. Each source requires its own locator, digest, extraction record, and evidentiary classification.### 1.3 Provenance requirementsEvery ingested artifact should preserve:- Immutable `sourceId`.- Source locator.- Acquisition timestamp.- Document version.- Cryptographic digest.- Extraction method.- Transformation history.- Human-review status.- Epistemic classification.- Relationship to other source records.The intended integrity mechanism is an RFC 8785-aligned canonical representation followed by a SHA-256 digest.---## 2. System Identity### 3.1 Libertas analytical latticeThe source describes a `LIBERTAS` analytical lattice intended to support intelligence ingestion, graph construction, provenance preservation, and epistemic control.The architecture combines:- Local language-model processing.- Retrieval-augmented generation.- Structured document extraction.- Vector indexing.- Neo4j graph representation.- Conceptual GeoJSON overlays.- Immutable provenance records.- Human-supervised review queues.- AEQUITAS interdiction rules.### 3.2 OODA operating modelThe system uses an OODA-inspired sequence.#### ObserveCollect:- arXiv PDFs.- OSINT records.- Public AIS data.- Satellite imagery.- Government reports.- Public financial and sanctions information.- Conceptual maritime layers.- Restricted or controlled GeoJSON inputs where authorized.The observation stage must not silently convert collection into validation.#### OrientInterpret source material through defined analytical modes:- Divergent analysis.- Convergent analysis.- Skeptical analysis.- Algorithmic analysis.- Spatial analysis.- Provenance analysis.The orientation stage must preserve uncertainty and distinguish between:- Direct observation.- Reported observation.- Model-derived inference.- Similarity-based retrieval.- Speculative interpretation.- Unverified assertion.#### DecideRoute analytical packages through layered review, including:- Planner review.- Critic review.- Provenance review.- Security review.- Policy review.- AEQUITAS interdiction review.The decision stage must not promote an unverified proposition to a verified fact without explicit human approval.#### ActThe ACT Controller may execute only authorized, reviewable operations.Permitted outputs include:- Dry-run SQL migrations.- Conceptual GeoJSON overlays.- Human-supervised review queues.- Records configured with `human_review_required: true`.Operational actions involving external systems require separate authorization, logging, and auditability.---## 4. ARXIV-MD-INGEST-V1 Contract### 4.1 PurposeThe `ARXIV-MD-INGEST-V1` contract converts raw arXiv PDFs and related source artifacts into structured Markdown while preserving:- Semantic hierarchy.- Section boundaries.- Tables.- Citations.- Mathematical notation.- Image references.- Entity mentions.- Provenance metadata.- Epistemic labels.- Source-to-claim relationships.### 4.2 Processing pipeline1. **Collect**     Acquire source artifacts from approved inputs.2. **Extract**     Parse text, tables, entities, figures, captions, citations, and mathematical notation.3. **Canonicalize**     Apply canonical JSON or JSON-LD representation suitable for deterministic hashing.4. **Enrich**     Identify geographic entities, temporal references, source relationships, and analytical classifications.5. **Index**     Store vector and graph representations in approved systems.6. **Store**     Preserve source artifacts and intermediate fragments in immutable or access-controlled storage.7. **Review**     Route uncertain or high-impact claims to human review.8. **Publish**     Export Markdown only after provenance and epistemic checks are complete.### 4.3 Example storage referenceThe source document provides the following example path:`source/arxiv/2605.25665/v1/source.pdf`This path is retained as a source-reported storage reference and should not be interpreted as evidence that the referenced object remains available or unchanged.---## 5. Rendering of Strategic Dynamics![Rendering of Strategic Dynamics maritime intelligence overlay](renderings-of-strategic-dynamics.png)### 5.1 Visual purposeThe visualization provides a contextual spatial fabric for maritime geography, global shipping routes, and crude-oil transit corridors.It is not intended to establish:- Attribution.- Command and control.- Operational responsibility.- Causal relationships.- Individual vessel activity.- Confirmed threat-actor infrastructure.- Confirmed smart-contract deployment.- Live operational tracking.### 5.2 Spatial context layerThe white-hot illuminated geography represents contextual maritime geography, including:- Red Sea.- Gulf of Aden.- Bab el-Mandeb.- Gulf of Oman.- Strait of Hormuz.- Persian Gulf.- Arabian Sea.- Suez Canal.- Regional ports.- Oil terminals.- Naval bases.- Major sea corridors.The illuminated geography is a spatial context layer. Brightness, proximity, or visual prominence must not be interpreted as a risk score or evidence of activity.### 5.3 Maritime transit layerBlue vectors represent conceptual or historically observed maritime transit patterns.They may illustrate:- Primary sea lanes.- Alternate routes.- Crude-oil transit corridors.- Commercial traffic.- Tanker traffic.- Bulk-carrier traffic.- Naval traffic.- Fishing-vessel traffic.The blue vectors describe movement through a maritime system. They do not assert that a specific vessel traveled along a depicted path at a particular time.### 5.4 Temporal separationHistorical or analytical route displacement may be represented as an overlay, provided that it is clearly separated from live operational tracking.A route display should identify whether it represents:- Historical movement.- Statistical aggregation.- Conceptual modeling.- Publicly documented transit.- Forecasting.- Live or near-real-time data.Absent a validated timestamp and source, the display must not be described as live tracking.---## 6. Epistemic Visual GrammarThe visualization follows the following color and symbol grammar.| Visual element | Meaning | Prohibited interpretation ||---|---|---|| White-hot geography | Spatial reality and contextual geography | Not a threat score || Blue transit vectors | Maritime transit context | Not proof of individual vessel activity || Red hollow nodes | Unverified source assertions | Not verified infrastructure || Solid markers | Evidence-backed observations with provenance | Must include source support || Red X | Interdiction or prohibited epistemic promotion | Not an operational attack indicator || Quarantine symbol | Isolated or unresolved claim | Not evidence of guilt or capability |### 6.1 Red hollow nodesRed hollow nodes may represent source assertions concerning:- Threat-actor infrastructure.- Reported organizational relationships.- Alleged cyber units.- Alleged ballistic capabilities.- Unverified smart-contract deployments.- Unverified network relationships.- Unconfirmed operational capabilities.Examples from the source visualization include:- IRGC-related nodes.- IRGC-associated regional nodes.- Cyber-unit nodes.- A node labeled `WLFI Aave v3 Pool Proxy`.- Other nodes marked `UNVERIFIED`.These nodes are intentionally hollow or quarantined. They indicate that a claim exists in the source material, not that the claim is true.### 6.2 Solid and verified markersA solid marker may be used only when:1. The observation has a traceable source.2. The source is appropriate for the proposition.3. The observation has a stable locator.4. The extraction and transformation process is recorded.5. The claim has passed the required review threshold.A marker's visual solidity must not exceed the strength of its provenance.### 6.3 Route intersection constraintA maritime route intersecting a node does not establish:- Attribution.- Command and control.- Causality.- Financial linkage.- Operational responsibility.- Shared ownership.- Deliberate coordination.Formally:`route_intersection ≠ attribution``graph_proximity ≠ causality``model_similarity ≠ corroboration``governance_proposal ≠ deployed_protocol`---## 7. AEQUITAS RuleChain Interdiction### 7.1 Governance precedence hierarchyThe source framework defines the following precedence order:1. Human-approved policy.2. `AGENTS.md`.3. AEQUITAS Gate.4. Task contract.5. `CHAIN.md` dispatch.6. Model preference.Where conflicts occur, higher-precedence rules govern.### 7.2 Deterministic interdiction triggers| Trigger code | Condition | Required disposition ||---|---|---|| `INCOMPLETE_PROVENANCE` | Payload lacks immutable `sourceId` or `sourceLocator` | Route to quarantine || `SIMILARITY_IS_NOT_CORROBORATION` | Match is derived solely from vector distance or embedding rank | Strip evidentiary weight || `MARKET_SIGNAL_IS_NOT_EVENT_EVIDENCE` | Speculative pricing or token-forum debate is used as event proof | Tag as `SENTIMENT_ONLY` || `GENERATED_SUMMARY_IS_NOT_SOURCE_EVIDENCE` | LLM synthesis is presented as primary documentation | Reject verbatim attribution || `PROHIBITED_CAUSAL_RELATIONSHIP` | Edge matches a causal class such as `CONTROLS`, `FINANCES`, or `EXECUTES_C2` without adequate evidence | Downgrade to `RELATED_TO` || `SILENT_EPISTEMIC_UPGRADE` | Unverified proposition is promoted to `VERIFIED_FACT` without human sign-off | Quarantine and require review |### 7.3 Mindset selection matrix#### Spatial modeValidates:- Topology.- Polygon coordinates in `[longitude, latitude]` order.- Geometry validity.- Non-navigational integrity.- `navigation_suitability = 0` for conceptual or analytical layers that are not intended for navigation.#### Convergent modeSeparates:- Municipal legal baselines.- Primary legal texts.- Secondary governmental analysis.- News reporting.- Open-source commentary.- Model-generated synthesis.#### Skeptical modeTests:- Whether evidence actually supports the proposition.- Whether source independence exists.- Whether apparent corroboration is merely duplicated reporting.- Whether graph proximity is being mistaken for causality.- Whether a visualization is promoting an unverified assertion.#### Algorithmic modeChecks:- Schema compliance.- Hash consistency.- Coordinate validity.- Deterministic transformations.- Relationship-type constraints.- Required review fields.- Provenance completeness.---## 8. The claim must be linked to the specific source artifact and relevant passage, record, coordinate, timestamp, or observation.---## 11. Provenance and Governance Model### 11.1 Evidence hierarchyThe architecture should distinguish the following layers:1. **Source artifact**     Original PDF, dataset, image, report, record, or document.2. **Extracted observation**     Text, coordinate, timestamp, entity, table row, or image feature extracted from the source.3. **Analytical feature**     A normalized or derived feature generated from one or more observations.4. **Relationship**     A graph edge whose semantics are constrained by evidence.5. **Hypothesis**     An analytical proposition requiring validation.6. **Disposition**     Relevant, contextual, quarantined, rejected, or verified.7. **Decision**     A human-reviewed determination that may authorize a higher-confidence classification.### 11.2 No silent upgradeThe system must prevent automatic transitions such as:`UNVERIFIED → VERIFIED_FACT`unless a human reviewer has approved the transition and the review record includes:- Reviewer identity.- Review timestamp.- Evidence considered.- Reason for disposition.- Scope of the decision.- Any remaining uncertainty.### 11.3 Relationship restrictionsThe following relationship types require especially strong evidence:- `CONTROLS`- `FINANCES`- `EXECUTES_C2`- `DIRECTS`- `OWNS`- `OPERATES`- `COORDINATES`If evidence is insufficient, the relationship should be downgraded to a non-causal form such as:- `RELATED_TO`- `MENTIONED_WITH`- `COLOCATED_WITH`- `REPORTED_BY`- `TEMPORALLY_ASSOCIATED_WITH`- `HYPOTHESIZED_RELATIONSHIP`---## 12. Quantum Anchor MetaphorsThe source manuscript references three symbolic anchors.### 12.1 DragonThe Dragon symbol represents:- Mutation containment.- Parallel evolution.- Adversarial adaptation.- Separation of competing hypotheses.This is a conceptual metaphor, not a technical model or validated operational capability.### 12.2 KrakenThe Kraken or Oracle Eye represents:- Predictive intelligence.- Integrated data feeds.- Cross-source analysis.- Detection of emerging patterns.The metaphor does not establish predictive accuracy or source reliability.### 12.3 Golden AnchorThe Golden Anchor represents:- SHA-256 document integrity.- Immutable provenance.- Canonicalized source records.- Preservation of the evidence chain.A cryptographic hash confirms artifact integrity relative to a known digest. It does not confirm the truth of the artifact's contents.---## 13. Storage FrameworkThe source document proposes structured storage for:- Source artifacts.- Raw PDFs.- Extracted text.- Intermediate processing fragments.- Vector representations.- Graph records.- Review decisions.- Audit logs.- Conceptual geographic overlays.The source references Cloudflare R2 as an example object-storage framework.Storage infrastructure does not by itself establish:- Data accuracy.- Source authenticity.- Legal authority.- Operational validity.- Human approval.- Analytical correctness.Those properties require separate controls.---## 14. Review and Publication RequirementsBefore publication, each manuscript or analytical overlay should pass the following checks.### 14.1 Provenance checklist- [ ] Every major claim has a source identifier.- [ ] Every source has a stable locator.- [ ] Source version is recorded.- [ ] Acquisition date is recorded.- [ ] Cryptographic digest is recorded where applicable.- [ ] Extracted material is separated from analyst interpretation.- [ ] Generated summaries are not presented as original source text.- [ ] Unverified assertions are labeled.- [ ] Causal relationships have explicit supporting evidence.- [ ] Live, historical, conceptual, and forecast layers are separated.### 14.2 Spatial checklist- [ ] Coordinates use `[longitude, latitude]` order.- [ ] Geographic features are valid.- [ ] Conceptual overlays are labeled as non-navigational.- [ ] Route vectors do not imply individual vessel activity.- [ ] Spatial proximity is not treated as causality.- [ ] Red hollow nodes remain visually distinct from verified observations.### 14.3 Human-review checklist- [ ] High-impact assertions require human review.- [ ] Relationship upgrades require explicit approval.- [ ] Rejected and quarantined claims remain traceable.- [ ] Review decisions are immutable or audit-logged.- [ ] No external operation is represented as completed without an execution record.---## 15. Outcome: Knowledge as a Living LatticeThe combined document and imagery describe a layered analytical model:`Evidence → Features → Relationships → Knowledge`The model is governed by the following constraints:- Evidence precedes inference.- Provenance precedes promotion.- Similarity does not equal corroboration.- Proximity does not equal causality.- Connectivity does not equal command and control.- Market sentiment does not equal event evidence.- A generated summary does not equal a primary source.- A governance proposal does not equal a deployed protocol.- A route intersection does not establish responsibility.- Model confidence does not equal truth.- Unverified claims must remain unverified until reviewed.The intended result is an analytical system that can represent a claim without visually or procedurally promoting it.> **MAP THE CLAIM — NEVER PROMOTE THE CLAIM.**---## 16. RED X / QUARANTINE = prohibited epistemic promotion

MAP THE CLAIM — NEVER PROMOTE THE CLAIM.

## 5. Epistemic Classification


### 5.1 Classification vocabulary


| Status | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `SOURCE_MENTION` | The entity or proposition appears in a source |
| `REPORTED_ASSERTION` | A source reports the proposition |
| `DERIVED_FEATURE` | The proposition was generated through extraction or computation |
| `HYPOTHESIS` | The proposition is analytically plausible but unverified |
| `QUARANTINED` | The proposition cannot be used for promotion or graph mutation |
| `VALIDATED_OBSERVATION` | Evidence supports a bounded observation |
| `VERIFIED_FACT` | Human-reviewed and adequately supported proposition |
| `REJECTED` | Unsupported, conflated, or prohibited proposition |


### 5.2 Current disposition


All Variant D propositions in this record are initially classified as:


```
QUARANTINED
```


No item may be classified as `VERIFIED_FACT` from:


- Model output alone.
- Vector similarity alone.
- Search-result snippets alone.
- User-interface labels alone.
- Repeated secondary summaries alone.
- Governance language alone.
- Co-occurrence in a graph.
- A visual node or route intersection.

## 6. Financial-Domain Entity Review


### 6.1 Entities requiring disambiguation


The following names or labels require independent identity resolution:


- WLFI
- Aave
- Aave v3
- `WLFI Aave v3 Pool Proxy`
- Kraken
- Hugging Face
- Llama
- Gemini
- Any associated wallet, contract, organization, foundation, protocol, or user account


Identity resolution must distinguish among:


- Legal entity.
- Brand.
- Protocol.
- Smart contract.
- Wallet address.
- Governance proposal.
- Deployment artifact.
- User account.
- Model or platform.
- News or commentary reference.


### 6.2 Required validation evidence


Before any financial-domain relationship is promoted, reviewers must obtain appropriate primary evidence, such as:


- Official legal or organizational records.
- Official protocol documentation.
- Verifiable contract address.
- Chain identifier.
- Deployment transaction.
- Block number or timestamp.
- Bytecode or verified source code.
- Governance proposal identifier.
- Final governance vote.
- Execution transaction.
- Official account or documentation linking the entity to the deployment.
- Independent corroboration from appropriately distinct sources.


A name match or model-generated mapping is insufficient.


### 6.3 Restricted relationship predicates


The following predicates remain prohibited until explicitly validated:


```
OWNSCONTROLSFINANCESOPERATESDEPLOYEDAUTHORIZED_BYEXECUTESGOVERNSDIRECTSCOORDINATES
```


If a relationship is retained for analytical continuity, it must use a non-causal predicate such as:


```
MENTIONED_WITHREPORTED_BYPOSSIBLY_RELATED_TOHYPOTHESIZED_RELATIONSHIPREQUIRES_VALIDATION
```

## 7. Smart-Contract Governance Review


### 7.1 Prohibited inference


The following inference is prohibited:


```
governance proposal → deployed protocol
```


A proposal, forum discussion, token reference, documentation page, or model summary does not establish that a contract was deployed or executed.


### 7.2 Required deployment validation


A smart-contract deployment claim requires:


1. Network or chain identification.
2. Contract address.
3. Deployment transaction hash.
4. Block number.
5. Deployment timestamp.
6. Deployer address.
7. Verified bytecode or source, where available.
8. Contract ABI, where relevant.
9. Proxy and implementation relationships.
10. Upgrade administrator or governance authority.
11. Evidence that the deployment corresponds to the named entity.
12. Independent review of the interpretation.


Absent these fields, the claim remains:


```
UNVERIFIED_SMART_CONTRACT_ASSERTION
```


### 7.3 Proxy-specific caution


A label such as `Pool Proxy` does not, by itself, prove:


- Protocol ownership.
- Governance control.
- Operational use.
- Financial activity.
- Relationship to WLFI.
- Relationship to Aave.
- Relationship to Kraken.
- Authorization by any named organization.


The label must be treated as an unverified source assertion until validated against chain-level evidence.


---


## 8. User-Onboarding Claims


### 8.1 Review requirement


Claims concerning user onboarding must not be interpreted as proof of:


- Institutional membership.
- Authorized access.
- Beneficial ownership.
- Platform affiliation.
- Financial control.
- Identity verification.
- Operational participation.


### 8.2 Required evidence


A user-onboarding claim requires:


- Source identity.
- Exact wording.
- Context.
- Date and time.
- Platform or application.
- Whether the statement was official, user-generated, automated, or model-generated.
- Independent confirmation.
- Scope of any authorization.
- Evidence separating account creation from institutional endorsement.


Until validated, onboarding-related statements remain:


```
UNVETTED_USER_ONBOARDING_CLAIM
```

### 9.1 Hugging Face references


The submitted material references Hugging Face and model pages. These references are retained as source mentions.


A model-hosting page may establish that a page or model record exists, but it does not automatically establish:

## 9. Detected Conflation Risks


| Risk | Description | Disposition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Source/model conflation | Model names and platform pages are treated as evidence of claim validity | Quarantine |
| Access/status conflation | A reported access outcome is treated as a universal or current platform rule | Validate timestamp and source |
| Citation conflation | Cited AI answers are treated as original source evidence | Strip evidentiary weight |
| Maritime/operational conflation | Route references are interpreted as live activity or responsibility | Retain as context only |
| Entity conflation | Model, platform, organization, and user identity are merged | Require entity resolution |
| Workflow/result conflation | A proposed ingestion workflow is presented as completed execution | Require execution record |
| Similarity conflation | Semantic closeness is treated as corroboration | Block |
| Governance conflation | A proposed control or rule is treated as deployed enforcement | Block |
| Visualization conflation | A node or label is interpreted as a verified fact | Retain as unverified assertion |


---


## 10. AEQUITAS Interdiction Rules


The following rules apply to this record:


| Trigger | Condition | Action |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `INCOMPLETE_PROVENANCE` | Missing immutable source identity or locator | Keep in quarantine |
| `GENERATED_SUMMARY_IS_NOT_SOURCE_EVIDENCE` | AI answer or summary presented as primary documentation | Reject attribution |
| `SIMILARITY_IS_NOT_CORROBORATION` | Relationship inferred from vector or semantic similarity | Remove evidentiary weight |
| `PLATFORM_REFERENCE_IS_NOT_AUTHORIZATION` | Platform page treated as proof of access, endorsement, or affiliation | Require validation |
| `ROUTE_PROXIMITY_IS_NOT_CAUSALITY` | Spatial adjacency treated as responsibility | Downgrade to contextual relation |
| `SILENT_EPISTEMIC_UPGRADE` | Unverified claim rewritten as verified fact | Block and log |
| `UNAUTHORIZED_GRAPH_MUTATION` | Unreviewed entity or edge added to a graph | Prohibit mutation |
| `PROPOSAL_IS_NOT_DEPLOYMENT` | Governance proposal treated as deployed protocol | Require deployment evidence |


---

Before any claim is considered for promotion, reviewers must establish:


- The exact source artifact.
- A stable source locator.
- Source date and version.
- Relevant quoted passage or record.
- Entity identity.
- Claim scope.
- Temporal scope.
- Geographic scope, where applicable.
- Whether the claim is direct observation, report, inference, or speculation.
- Independent corroboration where required.
- Any conflict or ambiguity among sources.
- A complete review decision.

A claim may be considered for promotion only if:


```
source_id_present: truesource_locator_present: truesource_version_recorded: trueclaim_scope_defined: trueentity_identity_resolved: trueprovenance_preserved: trueconflation_review_complete: truehuman_review_complete: truegraph_mutation_authorized: true
```

```

Renderings.md
Renderings.md
LIBERTAS v6.10 // VERITAS // AEQUITAS
Strategic Visual Corridor Map & Verification Flow


System Core: ARXIV-MD-INGEST-V1
Security Governance: LIBERTAS v6.10 / AEQUITAS INTERDICTION GATE
Graph Model: SMCP — Structured Multi-Context Provenance
Primary Domain: Maritime Location Intelligence / OSINT Analytical Cartography
Theater: Strait of Hormuz · Persian Gulf · Gulf of Oman · Red Sea
Rendering Status: CONTEXTUAL / NON-CAUSAL / OSINT-SAFE
Execution Mode: DRY_RUN → HUMAN_REVIEW_REQUIRED


1. Core Graph-Governance Axiom


MAP THE CLAIM — NEVER PROMOTE THE CLAIM.


The rendering system may spatially represent an assertion, report, observation, or analytical hypothesis without converting that object into an established fact.


Every graph object remains:


explicitly typed;
provenance-bound;
temporally attributable;
confidence-scoped;
disposition-controlled;
subject to human review.


The visual layer therefore represents epistemic state, not merely geographic position.


Core invariant
Source
↓
Metadata
↓
Context
↓
Claim
↓
Provenance
↓
Review
↓
Disposition


Never:


Source → Fact → Graph Mutation
2. Information Asymmetry Layer


The strategic rendering separates three fundamentally different conditions.


2.1 Deterministic Evidence Substrate
WHITE-HOT CORE


The white-hot layer represents artifacts whose integrity and canonical representation can be established.


Source Artifact
↓
Canonicalization
↓
RFC 8785
↓
SHA-256
↓
Integrity Anchor


The cryptographic anchor establishes that the represented artifact has not been altered after canonicalization.


It does not establish that every assertion contained in the artifact is true.


Governing principle


Cryptographic certainty ≠ intelligence certainty.


A valid SHA-256 digest proves integrity of the artifact being hashed. It does not independently validate the underlying event, attribution, interpretation, or causal relationship.


2.2 Low-Observability Maritime Domain
DARK / DIFFUSE ZONE


The dark maritime layer represents:


incomplete collection;
sparse observation;
uncertain vessel identity;
interrupted AIS visibility;
ambiguous reporting;
informal networks;
uncertain spatial attribution;
incomplete temporal coverage.


The layer must not visually imply that low observability itself proves deliberate evasion.


Governing principle


Absence of evidence ≠ evidence of absence.


The rendering therefore distinguishes:


NOT OBSERVED


from:


DID NOT OCCUR
2.3 Information Asymmetry


The principal analytical insight is:


A perfectly authenticated source can still contain an unverified assertion, while real activity may occur inside a low-observability environment without the available evidence establishing that activity.


Therefore:


Artifact Integrity
≠
Event Validity
≠
Attribution
≠
Causality


This distinction is the epistemic backbone of the rendering.


1. Rendering Grammar
  Visual Element Epistemic Meaning Graph Treatment
  Red Hollow Node Unverified source assertion SOURCE_ASSERTION
  White-Hot Node/Core Integrity-anchored artifact EVIDENCE_ARTIFACT
  Blue Route Network Observed/contextual maritime connectivity OBSERVED_CONTEXT
  Dark/Diffuse Zone Collection uncertainty LOW_OBSERVABILITY
  SHA-256 Anchor Artifact integrity boundary INTEGRITY_ANCHOR
  AEQUITAS Wall Epistemic promotion barrier INTERDICTION_GATE
  SMCP Graph Provenance/context lattice PROVENANCE_GRAPH
  OODA Ring Analytical workflow OBSERVE → ORIENT → DECIDE → ACT
  Dashed Red Edge Proposed/unverified relationship PROPOSED_RELATIONSHIP
  Solid Causal Edge Prohibited unless independently adjudicated BLOCKED
2. Red Hollow Node Protocol
  Definition


A red hollow node represents an assertion that has entered the analytical graph as a claim, not as a verified fact.


Example classes include:


IRGC maritime influence
Quds Force maritime relationship
proxy-network logistics claim
smuggling-route assertion
governance proposal
market-related infrastructure claim


The rendering does not determine whether the assertion is true.


Required properties
node_type: SOURCE_ASSERTION
epistemic_status: UNVERIFIED
visual_state: RED_HOLLOW
provenance_required: true
human_review_required: true
causal_inference: prohibited
automatic_promotion: prohibited
Required routing
SOURCE_ASSERTION
↓
AEQUITAS
↓
┌─────────────────────────────┐
│ HUMAN REVIEW REQUIRED │
└─────────────────────────────┘
↓
QUARANTINE / PENDING_REVIEW
5. Blue Network Protocol


Blue network structures represent observed or contextual connectivity.


Examples:


publicly observable maritime lanes;
AIS-visible vessel traffic;
port-access geography;
commercial shipping relationships;
tanker corridors;
insurance/risk-market context;
publicly documented transportation networks.


A blue edge does not automatically mean:


CONTROL
OWNERSHIP
COMMAND
FINANCING
TARGETING
OPERATIONAL COORDINATION
Default relationship
OBSERVED_CONNECTIVITY


not:


CAUSAL_RELATIONSHIP
6. White-Hot Evidence Protocol


White-hot nodes represent evidence artifacts whose provenance or integrity has been established to the degree specified by their metadata.


Examples:


Canonicalized document
SHA-256 anchored artifact
Verified geospatial dataset
Timestamped public-source record
Reproducible transformation artifact
Important limitation
SHA-256
↓
Integrity


does not mean:


SHA-256
↓
Truth


The rendering must never visually imply that cryptographic anchoring validates an external-world proposition.

  corridor_strait_hormuz

The AEQUITAS wall is the visual and logical boundary between observation and graph mutation.

10. Blocked Transitions


The following transitions are prohibited by default:


Source Assertion → Verified Fact


Media Report → Confirmed Intelligence


Graph Proximity → Causality


Similarity → Corroboration


Market Sentiment → Event Occurrence


Model Confidence → Evidence Confidence


Governance Proposal → Deployed Protocol


Prediction Probability → Future Fact
Prohibited causal edges

OWNS
OPERATES_IN
RESPONSIBLE_FOR

FINANCES
EXECUTES_C2


Incoming causal claims should be represented as:


RELATED_TO


or stored in:


proposed_relationships


until independently adjudicated.

18. Neo4j Governance Semantics


A graph node should preserve epistemic state as first-class metadata.


Conceptual example:


(:Claim {
claim_id: “…”,
node_type: “SOURCE_ASSERTION”,
epistemic_status: “UNVERIFIED”,
provenance_required: true,
human_review_required: true
})


Permitted contextual relationship:


(:Claim)-[:RELATED_TO]->(:MaritimeCorridor)


Not automatically permitted:


(:Claim)-[:CONTROLS]->(:MaritimeCorridor)


or:


(:Entity)-[:EXECUTES_C2]->(:Vessel)


The second class requires independent adjudication and must not be inferred from graph topology.


1. Spatial Governance


Spatial visualization is contextual only.


navigation_suitability: 0
operational_targeting: false
c2_generation: false
route_optimization: false
target_selection: false


Coordinates, where used, should represent broad geographic context rather than actionable targeting information.


The rendering is an analytical cartography layer, not a navigation or targeting system.


1. Information-Asymmetry Doctrine


The system explicitly recognizes two asymmetric conditions:


Condition A
HIGH INTEGRITY
+
LOW EPISTEMIC VALIDITY


An authentic document can faithfully preserve an assertion that remains unverified.


Condition B
LOW OBSERVABILITY
+
POSSIBLE REAL-WORLD ACTIVITY


An absence of observable data does not establish that nothing occurred.


Therefore:


Integrity ≠ Truth
Visibility ≠ Existence
Similarity ≠ Corroboration
Proximity ≠ Causality
Probability ≠ Fact
Confidence ≠ Evidence

**When the external handshake with the Hugging Face hub was rejected, the** AEQUITAS **gate safely decoupled the task from external resources to execute local fallback logging:**


**### 1. Ingestion Pipeline Breakdown (Failed Proposal)**


**The system attempted to ingest an unstructured payload referencing the Strait of Hormuz, Red Sea, and Bab el-Mandeb shipping routes. Because the upstream pipeline model request was locked out, the provenance link could not build an unalterable object footprint inside the production database, resulting in a Missing Provenance Log fault.**


White-hot illuminated geography: Red Sea, Gulf of Aden, Gulf of Oman, Strait of Hormuz, Persian Gulf, Arabian Sea, and connected maritime geography.
Blue transit vectors: Conceptual shipping and crude-oil transit lanes showing movement through the maritime system rather than asserting individual vessel activity.
Primary spatial anchors: Suez Canal, Bab el-Mandeb, Strait of Hormuz, major ports, terminals, and regional sea corridors.
Temporal dimension: Route displacement and extended transit patterns can be represented as historical/analytical overlays, clearly separated from live operational tracking.
Epistemic separation: Spatial geography and publicly documented routes form the context layer; red hollow nodes remain unverified source assertions.
AEQUITAS constraint: A route intersecting a node does not establish attribution, command-and-control, causality, or operational responsibility.

exists

MAP THE CLAIM — NEVER PROMOTE THE CLAIM.

## I. Structural Domain Taxonomy & Operational Grammar


The domain ecosystem is segmented into four distinct operational functional tiers:


1. Cover & Social Engineering Tier (Recruitment & Job Portals)


Function:* Human-targeted reconnaissance, credential harvesting, and spear-phishing delivery.


Mechanism:* Mimics legitimate corporate HR portals, defense contractor career sites, and regional tech recruitment boards.


Epistemic Classification:* SOURCE_ASSERTION (Domains categorized as cover identity until direct victim telemetry establishes active interaction).


1. Influence & Ideological Tier (Religious & Cultural Fronts)


Function:* Soft-power projection, narrative dissemination, and localized audience profiling.


Mechanism:* Mirrors regional news aggregators, community forums, and religious outreach organization landing pages.


Epistemic Classification:* PROBABILISTIC_SIGNAL (Used for sentiment and influence mapping, tagged as SENTIMENT_ONLY).


1. Access Maintenance Tier (Generic Administrative Shells)


Function:* Staging secondary payloads, API proxying, and maintaining persistence behind benign domain names.


Mechanism:* Utilizes typosquatted SaaS platforms, cloud storage aliases, and innocuous utility domains.


Epistemic Classification:* UNVERIFIED_INFRASTRUCTURE.


1. Command & Control (*C*2) Relay Tier (Parallel Infrastructure Branches)


Function:* Low-velocity exfiltration and active session management.


Mechanism:* Disposable dynamic DNS endpoints and short-lived edge proxies routing traffic through compromised third-party networks.


—


## II. AEQUITAS Infrastructure Attribution Guardrails


To prevent false attribution and operational misclassification, the AEQUITAS Interdiction Gate enforces strict non-causal constraints on domain-to-actor linking:


“`text


DOMAIN OBSERVATION ──> [ AEQUITAS GATE ] ──> DOWNGRADE / QUARANTINE ──> HUMAN REVIEW


(Mundane Hostname) Evaluate Link (Non-Causal Relation)


“`


### Blocked Automatic Promotions


*Co-located IP Address** → Confirmed Shared Operator (Blocked)


*Similarity in Naming** → Attributed to Specific Actor Group (Blocked)


*Infrastructure Proximity** → Confirmed C2 Node (Blocked)


*Passive DNS Association** → Operational Attribution (Blocked)


### Prohibited Attribution Edge Classes


Without explicit telemetry or human analyst adjudication, the system MUST NOT automatically instantiate the following relationship edges:


Prohibited Domain Edges={OPERATED_BY,DIRECTS_CAMPAIGN,TARGETS_ENTITY,EXECUTES_C2}


### Allowed Non-Causal Relational Edges


Allowed Domain Edges={RESOLVES_TO,SHARE_REGISTRAR_WITH,THEMATICALLY_SIMILAR_TO,POTENTIAL_FRONT_FOR}


—


## III. Repository Quarantine & Rejection Logs (quarantine.md / rejected.md)


In compliance with the Defense Intelligence Agency (DIA) Request for Proposal (RFP) / Sources Sought framework and FedRAMP IL5 sandbox guidelines, unverified propositions and processing failures are logged deterministically:


### quarantine.md (Incident Repository Logs)


*INCOMPLETE_PROVENANCE (`INT-2026-08-11-001`):** Ingestion targets missing explicit source locators or valid cryptographic digests. Held pending manual locator attachment.


*SIMILARITY_IS_NOT_CORROBORATION (`INT-2026-08-11-002`):** Vector retrieval matches flagged when vector proximity attempts to override raw text verification.


*MARKET_SIGNAL_IS_NOT_EVENT_EVIDENCE (`INT-2026-08-11-003`):** Speculative prediction-market feeds (e.g., Polymarket maritime disruption pricing) quarantined to prevent event confirmation.


*SILENT_EPISTEMIC_UPGRADE (`INT-2026-08-11-004`):** Intercepted attempts by multi-agent reasoning loops to automatically promote conceptual threat actor domains into confirmed operational *C*2 infrastructure.


### rejected.md / llama-rejection.md (Repository Rejection Index)


*REJ-2026-001:** Submissions failing structural Markdown parsing validation or containing malformed LaTeX mathematical symbol syntax.


*REJ-2026-002:** Unverified external claims attempting to assign direct military command responsibility or attribution without multi-source intelligence corroboration.


*REJ-2026-003:** Data payloads violating air-gapped security boundaries or exhibiting active-content / prompt-injection signatures.


—


## IV. OODA Loop Integration in Defense Intelligence


The OODA loop (Observe, Orient, Decide, Act) functions as the core decision-making rhythm across the Libertas architecture. Originally framed by U.S. Air Force Colonel John Boyd, modern intelligence applications require adapting the loop to prevent automated hallucination and cognitive overreach:


1. Observe: Collects public OSINT corpora, arXiv preprints, and restricted GeoJSON layers while computing RFC 8785 SHA-256 cryptographic digests.
2. Orient: Leverages the Chain of Mindset (CoM) framework (Divergent, Convergent, Skeptical, and Algorithmic modes) bounded by the Bidirectional Context Gate to evaluate threat actor topologies without committing silent epistemic upgrades.
3. Decide: Passes analytical packages through the Multi-Agent Review Mesh (Planner, Critic, Provenance Reviewer, Security Reviewer, Policy Reviewer) and the AEQUITAS Interdiction Gate.
4. Act: Enforces deterministic execution via the ACT Controller, restricting operations to dry-run SQL migrations, conceptual GeoJSON overlays, and human-supervised review queues (`human_review_required: true`).

/*  AEQUITAS RED-HOLLOW NODE ISOLATION  System: ARXIV-MD-INGEST-V1  Mode: DRY_RUN_PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW  Purpose: Test-sandbox quarantine mutation only  Required parameters:  {    "sandbox_id": "NEMOTRON-3-NANO-OMNI-TEST",    "incident_id": "INCIDENT-L-2026-0914",    "execution_mode": "DRY_RUN_PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",    "reviewer": null,    "review_timestamp": null,    "node_ids": [      "GEO-ZONE-IRAN-BASELINE-1993",      "GEO-CLAIM-MODEL-ACCESS-REJECTION"    ]  }  Safety properties:  - Requires a :Sandbox node with environment = TEST.  - Requires execution_mode = DRY_RUN_PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW.  - Mutates only nodes in the named sandbox.  - Requires an existing RED_HOLLOW or unverified epistemic state.  - Does not delete nodes or relationships.  - Does not create causal, ownership, control, targeting, or C2 edges.*/// ---------------------------------------------------------------------// 1. Sandbox and execution-mode gate// ---------------------------------------------------------------------MATCH (sandbox:Sandbox {  sandbox_id: $sandbox_id,  environment: "TEST",  system: "ARXIV-MD-INGEST-V1"})WHERE $execution_mode = "DRY_RUN_PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW"WITH sandbox// ---------------------------------------------------------------------// 2. Create or reuse the incident quarantine record// ---------------------------------------------------------------------MERGE (incident:QuarantineIncident {  incident_id: $incident_id,  sandbox_id: $sandbox_id})ON CREATE SET  incident.system = "ARXIV-MD-INGEST-V1",  incident.framework = "LIBERTAS v6.10 / AEQUITAS",  incident.disposition = "FAILED_INGESTION_QUARANTINE",  incident.epistemic_status = "QUARANTINED",  incident.graph_mutation = "RESTRICTED",  incident.human_review_required = true,  incident.created_at = datetime()SET  incident.last_seen_at = datetime(),  incident.execution_mode = $execution_mode,  incident.source_reference = "meta-llama/Llama-3.1-8B",  incident.runtime_reference = "Nemotron 3 Nano Omni (free)",  incident.source_status = "SOURCE_REPORTED_ONLY"MERGE (sandbox)-[:CONTAINS_INCIDENT]->(incident)WITH sandbox, incident// ---------------------------------------------------------------------// 3. Select only explicitly unverified Red Hollow nodes// ---------------------------------------------------------------------UNWIND $node_ids AS requested_idMATCH (claim)WHERE claim.sandbox_id = $sandbox_id  AND (    claim.node_id = requested_id    OR claim.id = requested_id    OR claim.feature_id = requested_id  )  AND (    claim.visual_state = "RED_HOLLOW"    OR claim.validation_state IN [      "UNVERIFIED_ASSERTION",      "SOURCE_REPORTED_PENDING_REVIEW",      "QUARANTINED_CONFLATION_REVIEW"    ]    OR claim.epistemic_status IN [      "UNVERIFIED",      "SOURCE_ASSERTION",      "CONTEXT_SIGNAL",      "QUARANTINED"    ]  )WITH sandbox, incident, claim// ---------------------------------------------------------------------// 4. Preserve the prior state before applying quarantine labels// ---------------------------------------------------------------------SET claim.pre_quarantine_visual_state =      coalesce(claim.visual_state, "UNSPECIFIED"),    claim.pre_quarantine_validation_state =      coalesce(claim.validation_state, "UNSPECIFIED"),    claim.pre_quarantine_epistemic_status =      coalesce(claim.epistemic_status, "UNSPECIFIED"),    claim.quarantine_reason =      "RED_HOLLOW_UNVERIFIED_ASSERTION",    claim.epistemic_status = "QUARANTINED",    claim.validation_state = "QUARANTINED_PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",    claim.visual_state = "RED_HOLLOW",    claim.graph_mutation = "PROHIBITED",    claim.causal_edges_allowed = false,    claim.human_review_required = true,    claim.promotion_authorized = false,    claim.isolated = true,    claim.quarantined_at = datetime(),    claim.quarantine_incident_id = $incident_id,    claim.quarantine_sandbox_id = $sandbox_idSET claim:QuarantinedClaim// ---------------------------------------------------------------------// 5. Add only non-causal quarantine relationships// ---------------------------------------------------------------------MERGE (claim)-[q:QUARANTINED_UNDER]->(incident)ON CREATE SET  q.created_at = datetime(),  q.reason = "RED_HOLLOW_UNVERIFIED_ASSERTION",  q.epistemic_effect = "NO_PROMOTION",  q.graph_effect = "NO_CAUSAL_MUTATION"SET  q.review_status = "PENDING",  q.human_review_required = true,  q.provenance_required = true// ---------------------------------------------------------------------// 6. Record allowed governance metadata// ---------------------------------------------------------------------SET claim.allowed_relationships = [  "RELATED_TO",  "MENTIONED_WITH",  "REPORTED_BY",  "REQUIRES_VALIDATION"],claim.prohibited_relationships = [  "OWNS",  "CONTROLS",  "OPERATES",  "DIRECTS",  "TARGETS",  "FINANCES",  "DEPLOYED",  "AUTHORIZED_BY",  "EXECUTES",  "EXECUTES_C2",  "COORDINATES"]WITH DISTINCT sandbox, incident, claim// ---------------------------------------------------------------------// 7. Return an auditable mutation report// ---------------------------------------------------------------------RETURN  sandbox.sandbox_id AS sandbox_id,  incident.incident_id AS incident_id,  coalesce(claim.node_id, claim.id, claim.feature_id) AS isolated_node_id,  claim.epistemic_status AS epistemic_status,  claim.validation_state AS validation_state,  claim.visual_state AS visual_state,  claim.graph_mutation AS graph_mutation,  claim.promotion_authorized AS promotion_authorized,  claim.human_review_required AS human_review_required,  claim.quarantined_at AS quarantined_atORDER BY isolated_node_id;
```

SOURCE_REPORTED_CONTEXT→ PROVENANCE_INCOMPLETE→ CONFLATION_REVIEW→ QUARANTINED→ VALIDATION_REQUIRED→ EPISTEMIC_PROMOTION_NOT_AUTHORIZED
```


No Neo4j mutation has been executed. If represented in a graph, use only non-causal relationships such as `RELATED_TO`, `MENTIONED_WITH`, or `REQUIRES_VALIDATION`; do not create `OPERATES`, `CONTROLS`, `TARGETS`, or `EXECUTES_C2` edges.


You: DoD Impact Level 5 (IL5) compliance is governed by the DISA Cloud Computing Security Requirements Guide (SRG) rather than a specific Defense Intelligence Agency document.


Ask-Chat: Correct. **DoD Impact Level 5 (IL5) is governed primarily by DISA’s DoD Cloud Computing Security Requirements Guide (CC SRG)** and related DoD authorization requirements—not by a generic “DIA RFP / Sources Sought” document.


The earlier wording should be corrected as follows:


```
**Compliance context:** DoD Cloud Computing Security Requirements Guide (CC SRG), DISA  **Impact Level:** IL5  **Data context:** Controlled Unclassified Information (CUI), subject to applicable mission-owner and contract requirements  **Authorization context:** DoD cloud authorization / provisional authorization requirements, applicable DFARS clauses, and the responsible Authorizing Official’s determination  **Processing status:** DRY_RUN_PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW
```


The distinction is important:


- **DISA CC SRG:** Defines DoD cloud security and authorization expectations, including impact-level requirements.
- **DFARS and contract clauses:** Establish acquisition and contractor obligations for cloud services.
- **FedRAMP:** May provide a baseline or authorization foundation, but **FedRAMP authorization alone does not automatically establish DoD IL5 authorization**.
- **DIA RFP or Sources Sought materials:** May impose additional program-specific requirements, but they are not the governing source for IL5 generally.
- **FedRAMP IL5:** This is imprecise terminology in a DoD context; it is better to refer to **DoD IL5 under the DISA CC SRG** unless a particular document explicitly defines another usage.


DISA states that the CC SRG applies to DoD-provided cloud services, commercial cloud service providers, DoD programs acting as providers, DoD components, mission owners, and authorizing officials. ([cyber.mil](https://www.cyber.mil/dccs/?utm_source=openai)) DFARS Subpart 239.76 also ties DoD cloud-service acquisition to DISA authorization at the level appropriate to the requirement and the applicable CC SRG version. ([acq.osd.mil](https://www.acq.osd.mil/dpap/dars/dfars/html/current/239_76.htm?utm_source=openai))


A corrected quarantine record would therefore replace:


```
Compliance Framework: DIA RFP / Sources Sought & FedRAMP IL5 Sandbox
```


with:


```
Compliance Framework: DISA DoD Cloud Computing Security Requirements Guide (CC SRG), DoD IL5Additional Requirements: Applicable DFARS clauses, contract terms, mission-owner controls, and Authorizing Official determination
```


This terminology correction does **not** by itself establish that the sandbox, model runtime, database, or processing environment is IL5-authorized. That status requires separate authorization evidence.

Contextual Spatial Fabric
• White-hot illuminated geography: Red Sea, Gulf of Aden, Gulf of Oman, Strait of Hormuz, Persian Gulf, Arabian Sea, and connected maritime geography.
• Blue transit vectors: Conceptual shipping and crude-oil transit lanes showing movement through the maritime system rather than asserting individual vessel activity.

• Temporal dimension: Route displacement and extended transit patterns can be represented as historical/analytical overlays, clearly separated from live operational tracking.
• Epistemic separation: Spatial geography and publicly documented routes form the context layer; red hollow nodes remain unverified source assertions.
• AEQUITAS constraint: A route intersecting a node does not establish attribution, command-and-control, causality, or operational responsibility.
The visual grammar is therefore:
WHITE HOT = spatial reality/context
BLUE = maritime transit/contextual network
RED HOLLOW = unverified claim
SOLID/VERIFIED MARKERS = evidence-backed observation, only where provenance exists
RED X / QUARANTINE = prohibited epistemic promotion
Core rendering principle:
MAP THE CLAIM — NEVER PROMOTE THE CLAIM.
This keeps the maritime layer analytically useful while preventing proximity, route intersection, graph connectivity, or model inference from being visually mistaken for verified attribution.


Ask-Chat: ## Corrected cartographic analysis


The governing compliance reference should be identified as the **DISA DoD Cloud Computing Security Requirements Guide (CC SRG)**, not a Defense Intelligence Agency document. The CC SRG defines the DoD cloud security and authorization model; it does not validate maritime claims, vessel activity, attribution, or intelligence assessments. ([cyber.mil](https://www.cyber.mil/dccs/?utm_source=openai))


The map should therefore treat the CC SRG as a **governance and handling-context reference**, while the Strait of Hormuz, Red Sea, Gulf of Aden, and Bab el-Mandeb are represented as **generalized geographic and maritime-context layers**.

## Provenance and epistemic controls


Each externally sourced assertion should be stored separately from the geographic layer:


```
Claim├── claim_id├── source_locator├── source_type├── retrieval_timestamp├── quoted_or_paraphrased_text├── geographic_reference├── temporal_reference├── confidence├── corroboration_status├── reviewer_status└── epistemic_state
```


Recommended state transitions:


```
SOURCE_REPORTED→ PROVENANCE_INCOMPLETE→ CONFLATION_REVIEW→ QUARANTINED→ HUMAN_REVIEW→ VERIFIED_OBSERVATION
```


No claim should be promoted merely because it:


- Intersects a corridor
- Appears near a named port or waterway
- Is repeated by a language model
- Has a Neo4j relationship to another claim
- Appears in a retrieved document
- Shares a time window with a geographic feature


## Neo4j-safe representation


Use non-causal relationships only:


```
(:Claim)-[:MENTIONED_WITH]->(:GeoCorridor)(:Claim)-[:LOCATED_NEAR]->(:GeographicAnchor)(:Claim)-[:SUPPORTED_BY]->(:Source)(:Claim)-[:REQUIRES_VALIDATION]->(:ReviewQueue)(:Claim)-[:QUARANTINED_AS]->(:EpistemicState)
```


Do **not** create these relationships without separately validated evidence:


```
OPERATESCONTROLSTARGETSESCORTSATTACKSEXECUTES_C2OWNSATTRIBUTED_TO
```


The rejected LLaMA access request should be recorded only as **model-access provenance**, not as evidence about either maritime corridor. It has no evidentiary value for vessel movement, route activity, attribution, or the CC SRG compliance status.

### Minor corrections


1. **Use a single file extension**


```
strait-hormuz-red-sea-geo-corridors.geojson
```


rather than:


```
strait-hormuz-red-sea-geo-corridors.geojson.json
```


1. **Make the date explicit**


Because `2026-09-15` is a snapshot date, label it as:


```
snapshot_date: "2026-09-15"
```


1. **Record the CC SRG citation as provenance**


Add a source record rather than relying only on the text `cyber.mil`:


```
sources:  - source_id: "SRC-DISA-CCSRG"    publisher: "DISA / DoD Cyber Exchange"    title: "DoD Cloud Computing Security Requirements Guide"    locator: "https://www.cyber.mil/dccs/"    source_type: "official_government_reference"    use: "cloud-governance-and-authorization-context"    evidentiary_scope:      - "DoD cloud security requirements"      - "authorization context"    does_not_establish:      - "maritime activity"      - "vessel identity"      - "route attribution"      - "threat attribution"      - "IL5 authorization of this application"

MAP THE CLAIM — NEVER PROMOTE THE CLAIM

Ask-Chat: ## Disposition: quarantine, not graph-ready


This material should be treated as **untrusted source content and workflow metadata**, not as verified maritime intelligence or an authorization record.


```
record_status: "QUARANTINED"ingestion_status: "NOT_GRAPH_READY"operational_status: "DRY_RUN_PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW"graph_mutation: falsecausal_inference: falselive_tracking: falseepistemic_promotion: falserequired_action: "SOURCE_AND_SCHEMA_VALIDATION"
```


### Issues requiring correction


- The embedded GeoJSON is **malformed**:
  - Curly quotation marks are used instead of JSON quotes.
  - Commas and closing braces are missing.
  - The first feature is not closed before the next feature begins.
  - The Bab el-Mandeb geometry is incomplete.
- `SRC-IRAN-MARINE-ACT-1993` and `SRC-US-DOS-LIS-114-1994` are identifiers, not sufficient provenance. Add:
  - Exact title
  - Issuing authority
  - Publication date and edition
  - Stable source locator
  - Retrieval timestamp
  - Relevant page or section
  - Hash of the retrieved source, if available
- `validation_state: "CONVERGENT_CRITIQUE"` should not be used unless the underlying source and its legal scope have been independently reviewed.
- `variant_assignment: "Variant C (IRGC Oil Corpus / Network Flows)"` risks implying an actor or network association. It should remain quarantined unless supported by a separately documented source.
- “Failed the network validation check” is not reproducible without a defined validation rule, input set, and audit result.
- The alleged local fallback execution should be recorded as **reported pipeline telemetry**, not as independently verified execution.
- The CC SRG reference establishes only a **cloud-governance context**. It does not validate the maritime features, legal interpretations, model runtime, or IL5 authorization status.


### Safer feature dispositions


```
features:  GEO-ZONE-IRAN-BASELINE-1993:    status: "SOURCE_REPORTED_LEGAL_POSITION"    epistemic_state: "QUARANTINED_PENDING_SOURCE_VALIDATION"    allowed_relationships:      - "SUPPORTED_BY"      - "DESCRIBES"      - "LOCATED_NEAR"      - "REQUIRES_VALIDATION"  GEO-CRITIQUE-LIS-114:    status: "SOURCE_REPORTED_LEGAL_ANALYSIS"    epistemic_state: "QUARANTINED_PENDING_SOURCE_VALIDATION"    allowed_relationships:      - "SUPPORTED_BY"      - "ANALYZES"      - "CONTRASTS_WITH"      - "REQUIRES_VALIDATION"  GEO-CORRIDOR-BAB-EL-MANDEB:    status: "GENERALIZED_CONTEXTUAL_CORRIDOR"    epistemic_state: "CONTEXT_SIGNAL"    operational_use: false    attribution_allowed: false    causal_edges_allowed: false
```


### Neo4j safety rule


Do not execute `neo4j_quarantine_red_hollow.cypher` against a production database. If a local dry-run representation is needed, restrict it to idempotent quarantine labels and provenance fields:


```
MERGE (c:Claim {claim_id: $claim_id})SET c.epistemic_state = 'QUARANTINED',    c.validation_state = 'PENDING_SOURCE_VALIDATION',    c.operational_use = false,    c.causal_inference = false,    c.graph_mutation_authorized = false,    c.reviewer_status = 'HUMAN_REVIEW_REQUIRED'RETURN c;
```


Do not create `OPERATES`, `CONTROLS`, `TARGETS`, `ATTACKS`, `EXECUTES_C2`, `OWNS`, or `ATTRIBUTED_TO` edges from this material.


The controlling principle remains:


```
Evidence precedes inference.Provenance precedes promotion.Source → Fact → Graph mutation.
```


The supplied content currently supports only:


```
Source-reported material→ Schema/provenance defects→ Quarantine→ Human validation required→ No graph promotion authorized
```

The system attempted to ingest an unstructured payload referencing the Strait of Hormuz, Red Sea, and Bab el-Mandeb shipping routes. Ask-Chat: ## Review of the supplied `Renderings.md` and image


The Markdown specification is largely aligned with an **OSINT-safe, provenance-first design**, but the rendered image contains several contradictions that should be corrected before publication or graph ingestion.


### Critical contradictions


| Image element | Problem | Required correction |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `Review Posture: NO HUMAN REVIEW REQUIRED` | Directly contradicts `HUMAN_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true` and the Markdown doctrine | Change to **`HUMAN REVIEW REQUIRED BEFORE EPISTEMIC PROMOTION`** |
| `IRGC Red Hollow Nodes` | Can visually imply that IRGC-linked entities are established map objects rather than source assertions | Use **`SOURCE-REPORTED IRGC-RELATED ASSERTIONS — UNVERIFIED`** |
| `Maritime Threat Indicators` with `HIGH`, `MEDIUM`, and numeric counts | No source locator, timestamp, methodology, or confidence basis is visible | Remove, quarantine, or label each as **illustrative / unverified / not operational** |
| `Key Chokepoint Metrics` and “Transit Share” values | May be mistaken for authoritative current statistics | Add source, date, measurement definition, and uncertainty—or remove |
| Vessel icons and bright route vectors | Could be interpreted as live traffic or operational routing | Add a prominent label: **`CONCEPTUAL, NON-LIVE, NON-NAVIGATION-GRADE`** |
| `GSMT`, `UAV Activity`, `USV Observations`, `AIS Anomalies` | These appear to assert observed activity without provenance | Replace with neutral labels such as **`SOURCE-REPORTED SIGNALS — QUARANTINED`** |
| `RED X: GUARANTEE` | “Guarantee” is not the intended epistemic meaning | Change to **`RED X: PROMOTION BLOCKED / QUARANTINE`** |
| `AEQUITAS RULECHAIN INTERDICTION` | Fine as an internal governance label, but it should not imply an external certification | Add **`INTERNAL ANALYTICAL CONTROL — NOT AN AUTHORIZATION OR INTELLIGENCE DETERMINATION`** |
| `https://dl.dod.cyber.mil/nodes` | Repeated URL-like text may be mistaken for source validation | Attach exact source metadata separately; do not treat a URL alone as evidence |


## Recommended corrected status banner


Replace the image’s review-status text with:


```

Supported:

Ask-Chat: ## GraphRAG → OpenAI GPT-5.6 Mini


The processing pipeline can be formalized as follows. For accuracy, label the model stage **OpenAI GPT-5.6 Mini**, rather than “GPT-5.6 Sol.”


### Processing stages


#### Stage 1 — PDF acquisition


**Supported sources**


- arXiv papers
- GitHub documentation
- Internal research
- Government publications
- User-provided PDFs and documents


**Output**


```
artifact:  format: PDF  source_locator: required  acquisition_timestamp: required  content_hash: recommended  acquisition_status: RECEIVED
```


#### Stage 2 — Source preservation


- Preserve the original file unchanged.
- Extract document metadata.
- Record page count, author, title, dates, and source links.
- Compute a content hash where applicable.


```
ORIGINAL PDF→ METADATA RECORD→ CONTENT HASH→ PRESERVED SOURCE ARTIFACT
```


#### Stage 3 — Parsing and extraction


- Extract text, headings, tables, captions, links, and page references.
- Preserve page-level source locators.
- Flag OCR uncertainty and malformed content.
- Do not treat extracted text as independently verified.


```
parse_status: COMPLETE | PARTIAL | FAILEDocr_used: true | falsetable_extraction: VERIFIED | UNCERTAINsource_locators: page_and_section_required
```


#### Stage 4 — Markdown normalization


```
PDF→ STRUCTURED TEXT→ MARKDOWN→ SECTION METADATA→ SOURCE LOCATORS
```


Important distinction:


```
MARKDOWN ≠ ORIGINAL SOURCE
```


Markdown is a derived representation and must retain a relationship to the source document.


#### Stage 5 — Claim extraction


Create separate claim objects rather than embedding assertions directly into graph nodes.


```
claim:  claim_id: required  statement: required  source_locator: required  geographic_reference: optional  temporal_reference: optional  extraction_method: model_assisted  epistemic_state: SOURCE_REPORTED  validation_state: PENDING_REVIEW
```


#### Stage 6 — GraphRAG indexing


- Chunk source material.
- Generate embeddings or retrieval indexes.
- Link chunks to source documents and claims.
- Preserve provenance for every retrieved passage.
- Treat similarity as a retrieval signal, not corroboration.


```
SOURCE→ CHUNKS→ EMBEDDINGS / INDEX→ RETRIEVAL→ CLAIM CANDIDATES→ PROVENANCE REVIEW
```


#### Stage 7 — GPT-5.6 Mini analysis


The model may assist with:


- Summarization
- Entity and relationship candidate extraction
- Schema normalization
- Contradiction detection
- Provenance-gap detection
- Draft review queues
- Non-causal contextual classification


The model must not autonomously establish:


```
ATTRIBUTIONCAUSALITYOWNERSHIPCONTROLOPERATIONAL RESPONSIBILITY
```


#### Stage 8 — Human validation


Review each candidate claim for:


- Source authenticity
- Exact locator
- Temporal validity
- Geographic basis
- Entity resolution
- Independent corroboration
- Scope and uncertainty
- Whether the relationship is causal or merely contextual


```
EVIDENCE?PROVENANCE?TEMPORAL VALIDITY?ENTITY RESOLUTION?INDEPENDENT SUPPORT?
```


#### Stage 9 — Neo4j disposition


Allowed contextual relationships:


```
MENTIONED_WITHLOCATED_NEARSUPPORTED_BYREQUIRES_VALIDATIONQUARANTINED_ASRELATED_TO
```


Relationships requiring separately validated evidence:


```
OPERATESCONTROLSTARGETSESCORTSATTACKSEXECUTES_C2OWNSATTRIBUTED_TOFINANCESDIRECTS
```


#### Stage 10 — Final states


```
disposition_states:  - SOURCE_REPORTED  - PROVENANCE_INCOMPLETE  - CONFLATION_REVIEW  - QUARANTINED  - HUMAN_REVIEW  - VERIFIED_OBSERVATION
```


### Pipeline invariant


```
SOURCE→ PRESERVATION→ PARSING→ MARKDOWN→ CLAIM→ PROVENANCE→ GRAPH-RAG RETRIEVAL→ HUMAN REVIEW→ DISPOSITION
```


Never use:


```
SOURCE→ MODEL OUTPUT→ VERIFIED FACT→ AUTOMATED GRAPH MUTATION
```


### Recommended diagram footer


```
GRAPH-RAG IS A RETRIEVAL AND CONTEXT SYSTEM.MODEL OUTPUT IS NOT EVIDENCE.EVIDENCE PRECEDES INFERENCE.PROVENANCE PRECEDES PROMOTION.HUMAN REVIEW REQUIRED.
```

Use code with caution.Key Architectural Choices ExplodedWhy Markdown? Standard PDF readers flatten document hierarchies. Parsing documents through markdown converters like marker ensures tables, mathematical syntax (LaTeX), headers (#, ##), and bold terminology remain clean. This prevents chunks from losing their structural formatting during tokenization. FAISS (Facebook AI Similarity Search): Utilized via memory-safe native vectors. It establishes flat indexing matching L2 distance metrics for blindingly fast local similarities. llama-cpp-python: Used to interface with LLaMA models locally using GGUF quantization. This lets you run powerful models (like LLaMA-3) on standard hardware without relying on cloud APIs.
