Ask-Chat: The specification is **internally consistent and suitable for an OSINT-safe, non-operational map layer**. It preserves the key boundary: geographic context may be displayed, but route proximity or model output must not be treated as attribution or operational evidence.


### Minor corrections


1. **Use a single file extension**


```
strait-hormuz-red-sea-geo-corridors.geojson
```


rather than:


```
strait-hormuz-red-sea-geo-corridors.geojson.json
```


1. **Make the date explicit**


Because `2026-09-15` is a snapshot date, label it as:


```
snapshot_date: "2026-09-15"
```


1. **Record the CC SRG citation as provenance**


Add a source record rather than relying only on the text `cyber.mil`:


```
sources:  - source_id: "SRC-DISA-CCSRG"    publisher: "DISA / DoD Cyber Exchange"    title: "DoD Cloud Computing Security Requirements Guide"    locator: "https://www.cyber.mil/dccs/"    source_type: "official_government_reference"    use: "cloud-governance-and-authorization-context"    evidentiary_scope:      - "DoD cloud security requirements"      - "authorization context"    does_not_establish:      - "maritime activity"      - "vessel identity"      - "route attribution"      - "threat attribution"      - "IL5 authorization of this application"
```


1. **Strengthen verified-observation requirements**


```
verified_observation_requirements:  - traceable_source_locator  - retrieval_timestamp  - geographic_basis  - temporal_basis  - corroboration_record  - human_reviewer  - explicit_scope  - uncertainty_statement
```


1. **Clarify the rejected model-access record**


```
model_access_event:  provider: "Meta"  model: "Llama-3.1-8B"  event: "Access request rejected"  evidentiary_status: "NON_EVIDENTIARY_METADATA"  may_support:    - "model-access provenance"    - "workflow audit history"  may_not_support:    - "maritime movement"    - "vessel attribution"    - "route activity"    - "threat assessment"    - "CC SRG compliance"
```


### Recommended final status


```
disposition:  map_status: "APPROVED_FOR_CONTEXTUAL_RENDERING"  operational_use: false  navigation_use: false  live_tracking: false  epistemic_promotion: "PROHIBITED_WITHOUT_HUMAN_REVIEW"  graph_mutation: "PROHIBITED"  causal_edges: "PROHIBITED"  unresolved_claims: "QUARANTINED"
```


The core rule remains appropriate:


```
MAP THE CLAIM — NEVER PROMOTE THE CLAIM
```


No `OPERATES`, `CONTROLS`, `TARGETS`, `ATTACKS`, `EXECUTES_C2`, `OWNS`, or `ATTRIBUTED_TO` relationships should be created from this specification alone.

Ask-Chat: ## Disposition: quarantine, not graph-ready


This material should be treated as **untrusted source content and workflow metadata**, not as verified maritime intelligence or an authorization record.


```
record_status: "QUARANTINED"ingestion_status: "NOT_GRAPH_READY"operational_status: "DRY_RUN_PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW"graph_mutation: falsecausal_inference: falselive_tracking: falseepistemic_promotion: falserequired_action: "SOURCE_AND_SCHEMA_VALIDATION"
```


### Issues requiring correction


- The embedded GeoJSON is **malformed**:
  - Curly quotation marks are used instead of JSON quotes.
  - Commas and closing braces are missing.
  - The first feature is not closed before the next feature begins.
  - The Bab el-Mandeb geometry is incomplete.
- `SRC-IRAN-MARINE-ACT-1993` and `SRC-US-DOS-LIS-114-1994` are identifiers, not sufficient provenance. Add:
  - Exact title
  - Issuing authority
  - Publication date and edition
  - Stable source locator
  - Retrieval timestamp
  - Relevant page or section
  - Hash of the retrieved source, if available
- `validation_state: "CONVERGENT_CRITIQUE"` should not be used unless the underlying source and its legal scope have been independently reviewed.
- `variant_assignment: "Variant C (IRGC Oil Corpus / Network Flows)"` risks implying an actor or network association. It should remain quarantined unless supported by a separately documented source.
- “Failed the network validation check” is not reproducible without a defined validation rule, input set, and audit result.
- The alleged local fallback execution should be recorded as **reported pipeline telemetry**, not as independently verified execution.
- The CC SRG reference establishes only a **cloud-governance context**. It does not validate the maritime features, legal interpretations, model runtime, or IL5 authorization status.


### Safer feature dispositions


```
features:  GEO-ZONE-IRAN-BASELINE-1993:    status: "SOURCE_REPORTED_LEGAL_POSITION"    epistemic_state: "QUARANTINED_PENDING_SOURCE_VALIDATION"    allowed_relationships:      - "SUPPORTED_BY"      - "DESCRIBES"      - "LOCATED_NEAR"      - "REQUIRES_VALIDATION"  GEO-CRITIQUE-LIS-114:    status: "SOURCE_REPORTED_LEGAL_ANALYSIS"    epistemic_state: "QUARANTINED_PENDING_SOURCE_VALIDATION"    allowed_relationships:      - "SUPPORTED_BY"      - "ANALYZES"      - "CONTRASTS_WITH"      - "REQUIRES_VALIDATION"  GEO-CORRIDOR-BAB-EL-MANDEB:    status: "GENERALIZED_CONTEXTUAL_CORRIDOR"    epistemic_state: "CONTEXT_SIGNAL"    operational_use: false    attribution_allowed: false    causal_edges_allowed: false
```


### Neo4j safety rule


Do not execute `neo4j_quarantine_red_hollow.cypher` against a production database. If a local dry-run representation is needed, restrict it to idempotent quarantine labels and provenance fields:


```
MERGE (c:Claim {claim_id: $claim_id})SET c.epistemic_state = 'QUARANTINED',    c.validation_state = 'PENDING_SOURCE_VALIDATION',    c.operational_use = false,    c.causal_inference = false,    c.graph_mutation_authorized = false,    c.reviewer_status = 'HUMAN_REVIEW_REQUIRED'RETURN c;
```


Do not create `OPERATES`, `CONTROLS`, `TARGETS`, `ATTACKS`, `EXECUTES_C2`, `OWNS`, or `ATTRIBUTED_TO` edges from this material.


The controlling principle remains:


```
Evidence precedes inference.Provenance precedes promotion.Source → Fact → Graph mutation.
```


The supplied content currently supports only:


```
Source-reported material→ Schema/provenance defects→ Quarantine→ Human validation required→ No graph promotion authorized
```


You: Neo4j-safe representation
Use non-causal relationships only:


Copy
(:Claim)-[:MENTIONED_WITH]->(:GeoCorridor)
(:Claim)-[:LOCATED_NEAR]->(:GeographicAnchor)
(:Claim)-[:SUPPORTED_BY]->(:Source)
(:Claim)-[:REQUIRES_VALIDATION]->(:ReviewQueue)
(:Claim)-[:QUARANTINED_AS]->(:EpistemicState)
Do not create these relationships without separately validated evidence:


Copy
OPERATES
CONTROLS
TARGETS
ESCORTS
ATTACKS
EXECUTES_C2
OWNS
ATTRIBUTED_TO
The rejected LLaMA access request should be recorded only as model-access provenance, not as evidence about either maritime corridor. It has no evidentiary value for vessel movement, route activity, attribution, or the CC SRG compliance status.

version: “OSINT-SAFE-CARTOGRAPHY-V1”

compliance_context:

DISA describes the CC SRG as a security and authorization framework for DoD cloud use by DoD components, mission owners, authorizing officials, and cloud service providers. The authorization process and current document set are maintained through the DoD Cyber Exchange. (cyber.mil)


Generalized GeoJSON layer

Provenance and epistemic controls
Each externally sourced assertion should be stored separately from the geographic layer:


Copy
Claim
├── claim_id
├── source_locator
├── source_type
├── retrieval_timestamp
├── quoted_or_paraphrased_text
├── geographic_reference
├── temporal_reference
├── confidence
├── corroboration_status
├── reviewer_status
└── epistemic_state
Recommended state transitions:


Copy
SOURCE_REPORTED
→ PROVENANCE_INCOMPLETE
→ CONFLATION_REVIEW
→ QUARANTINED
→ HUMAN_REVIEW
→ VERIFIED_OBSERVATION
No claim should be promoted merely because it:


Intersects a corridor
Appears near a named port or waterway
Is repeated by a language model
Has a Neo4j relationship to another claim
Appears in a retrieved document
Shares a time window with a geographic feature

When the external handshake with the Hugging Face hub was rejected, the AEQUITAS gate safely decoupled the task from external resources to execute local fallback logging:


### 1. Ingestion Pipeline Breakdown (Failed Proposal)


The system attempted to ingest an unstructured payload referencing the Strait of Hormuz, Red Sea, and Bab el-Mandeb shipping routes. Because the upstream pipeline model request was locked out, the provenance link could not build an unalterable object footprint inside the production database, resulting in a Missing Provenance Log fault.

Ask-Chat: ## GraphRAG → OpenAI GPT-5.6 Mini


The processing pipeline can be formalized as follows. For accuracy, label the model stage **OpenAI GPT-5.6 Mini**, rather than “GPT-5.6 Sol.”


### Processing stages


#### Stage 1 — PDF acquisition


**Supported sources**


- arXiv papers
- GitHub documentation
- Internal research
- Government publications
- User-provided PDFs and documents


**Output**


```
artifact:  format: PDF  source_locator: required  acquisition_timestamp: required  content_hash: recommended  acquisition_status: RECEIVED
```


#### Stage 2 — Source preservation


- Preserve the original file unchanged.
- Extract document metadata.
- Record page count, author, title, dates, and source links.
- Compute a content hash where applicable.


```
ORIGINAL PDF→ METADATA RECORD→ CONTENT HASH→ PRESERVED SOURCE ARTIFACT
```


#### Stage 3 — Parsing and extraction


- Extract text, headings, tables, captions, links, and page references.
- Preserve page-level source locators.
- Flag OCR uncertainty and malformed content.
- Do not treat extracted text as independently verified.


```
parse_status: COMPLETE | PARTIAL | FAILEDocr_used: true | falsetable_extraction: VERIFIED | UNCERTAINsource_locators: page_and_section_required
```


#### Stage 4 — Markdown normalization


```
PDF→ STRUCTURED TEXT→ MARKDOWN→ SECTION METADATA→ SOURCE LOCATORS
```


Important distinction:


```
MARKDOWN ≠ ORIGINAL SOURCE
```


Markdown is a derived representation and must retain a relationship to the source document.


#### Stage 5 — Claim extraction


Create separate claim objects rather than embedding assertions directly into graph nodes.


```
claim:  claim_id: required  statement: required  source_locator: required  geographic_reference: optional  temporal_reference: optional  extraction_method: model_assisted  epistemic_state: SOURCE_REPORTED  validation_state: PENDING_REVIEW
```


#### Stage 6 — GraphRAG indexing


- Chunk source material.
- Generate embeddings or retrieval indexes.
- Link chunks to source documents and claims.
- Preserve provenance for every retrieved passage.
- Treat similarity as a retrieval signal, not corroboration.


```
SOURCE→ CHUNKS→ EMBEDDINGS / INDEX→ RETRIEVAL→ CLAIM CANDIDATES→ PROVENANCE REVIEW
```


#### Stage 7 — GPT-5.6 Mini analysis


The model may assist with:


- Summarization
- Entity and relationship candidate extraction
- Schema normalization
- Contradiction detection
- Provenance-gap detection
- Draft review queues
- Non-causal contextual classification


The model must not autonomously establish:


```
ATTRIBUTIONCAUSALITYOWNERSHIPCONTROLOPERATIONAL RESPONSIBILITY
```


#### Stage 8 — Human validation


Review each candidate claim for:


- Source authenticity
- Exact locator
- Temporal validity
- Geographic basis
- Entity resolution
- Independent corroboration
- Scope and uncertainty
- Whether the relationship is causal or merely contextual


```
EVIDENCE?PROVENANCE?TEMPORAL VALIDITY?ENTITY RESOLUTION?INDEPENDENT SUPPORT?
```


#### Stage 9 — Neo4j disposition


Allowed contextual relationships:


```
MENTIONED_WITHLOCATED_NEARSUPPORTED_BYREQUIRES_VALIDATIONQUARANTINED_ASRELATED_TO
```


Relationships requiring separately validated evidence:


```
OPERATESCONTROLSTARGETSESCORTSATTACKSEXECUTES_C2OWNSATTRIBUTED_TOFINANCESDIRECTS
```


#### Stage 10 — Final states


```
disposition_states:  - SOURCE_REPORTED  - PROVENANCE_INCOMPLETE  - CONFLATION_REVIEW  - QUARANTINED  - HUMAN_REVIEW  - VERIFIED_OBSERVATION
```


### Pipeline invariant


```
SOURCE→ PRESERVATION→ PARSING→ MARKDOWN→ CLAIM→ PROVENANCE→ GRAPH-RAG RETRIEVAL→ HUMAN REVIEW→ DISPOSITION
```


Never use:


```
SOURCE→ MODEL OUTPUT→ VERIFIED FACT→ AUTOMATED GRAPH MUTATION
```


### Recommended diagram footer


```
GRAPH-RAG IS A RETRIEVAL AND CONTEXT SYSTEM.MODEL OUTPUT IS NOT EVIDENCE.EVIDENCE PRECEDES INFERENCE.PROVENANCE PRECEDES PROMOTION.HUMAN REVIEW REQUIRED.
```

Use code with caution.Key Architectural Choices ExplodedWhy Markdown? Standard PDF readers flatten document hierarchies. Parsing documents through markdown converters like marker ensures tables, mathematical syntax (LaTeX), headers (#, ##), and bold terminology remain clean. This prevents chunks from losing their structural formatting during tokenization. FAISS (Facebook AI Similarity Search): Utilized via memory-safe native vectors. It establishes flat indexing matching L2 distance metrics for blindingly fast local similarities. llama-cpp-python: Used to interface with LLaMA models locally using GGUF quantization. This lets you run powerful models (like LLaMA-3) on standard hardware without relying on cloud APIs. import os
import re
import sys
import logging
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Generator
import arxiv
import faiss
import numpy as np
from marker.convert import convert_single_pdf
from marker.models import load_all_models
from llama_cpp import Llama
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer


# Configure production logger


logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=’%(asctime)s – %(levelname)s – %(message)s’)
logger = logging.getLogger(“ARXIV_INGEST_CONTRACT”)


class IngestionContractPipeline:
def **init**(self, model_path: str, embedding_model: str = “all-MiniLM-L6-v2”):
“””Initializes LLaMA-aware components and multi-stage layout extraction tools.”””
self.output_dir = “./ingested_payloads”
os.makedirs(self.output_dir, exist_ok=True)


```
   logger.info("Initializing high-fidelity layout extraction architecture...")
    # Pre-loading Marker Layout & Segment models ensures predictable compute usage
    self.conversion_models = load_all_models()
    
    logger.info(f"Loading local semantic text embedding vectorizer: {embedding_model}")
    self.embedder = SentenceTransformer(embedding_model)
    self.faiss_index = None
    self.memory_chunks = []
    
    logger.info(f"Loading LLaMA Neural Engine from target path: {model_path}")
    self.llm = Llama(
        model_path=model_path,
        n_ctx=8192,         # Expanded context window for handling long academic papers
        n_gpu_layers=-1,    # Offload all processing layers if CUDA/MPS is detected
        n_threads=8,        # Scale according to your hardware core configuration
        verbose=False
    )

def extract_arxiv_payload(self, arxiv_id: str) -> str:
    """Fetches the target PDF via the public arXiv API and saves it safely to disk."""
    logger.info(f"Executing secure handshake with arXiv API for ID: {arxiv_id}")
    client = arxiv.Client()
    search = arxiv.Search(id_list=[arxiv_id])
    
    try:
        paper = next(client.results(search))
    except StopIteration:
        logger.error(f"Target payload extraction failed. ID {arxiv_id} not found.")
        sys.exit(1)
        
    pdf_path = paper.download_pdf(dirpath=self.output_dir, filename=f"{arxiv_id}.pdf")
    logger.info(f"Payload extraction successful: {pdf_path}")
    return pdf_path

def parse_pdf_to_structured_markdown(self, pdf_path: str) -> str:
    """Converts unstructured binary text streams into a standard Markdown layout."""
    logger.info("Converting unstructured document formatting into valid Markdown syntax...")
    # Marker strips page numbers, preserves equations, and tables
    markdown_text, _, _ = convert_single_pdf(pdf_path, self.conversion_models)
    
    # Save output data to guarantee traceability
    base_name = os.path.basename(pdf_path).replace(".pdf", ".md")
    md_out_path = os.path.join(self.output_dir, base_name)
    with open(md_out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as file_pointer:
        file_pointer.write(markdown_text)
        
    logger.info(f"Markdown file generated and saved: {md_out_path}")
    return markdown_text

def semantic_structural_chunking(self, md_text: str, max_tokens: int = 750) -> List[str]:
    """Chunks documents based on structural Markdown headers to keep contextual data intact."""
    logger.info("Running chunking algorithm to preserve header hierarchies...")
    # Split text along markdown section boundaries (# through ####)
    raw_sections = re.split(r'(^#+\s.*$)', md_text, flags=re.MULTILINE)
    
    chunks = []
    current_buffer = ""
    
    for segment in raw_sections:
        if not segment.strip():
            continue
        # If buffer size limits are breached, commit buffer to chunks list
        if len(current_buffer) + len(segment) > max_tokens and current_buffer:
            chunks.append(current_buffer.strip())
            current_buffer = segment
        else:
            current_buffer += "\n" + segment
            
    if current_buffer:
        chunks.append(current_buffer.strip())
        
    self.memory_chunks = chunks
    return chunks

def build_vector_store(self, chunks: List[str]):
    """Generates embeddings and builds a local FAISS vector store index."""
    logger.info(f"Generating dense matrix embeddings for {len(chunks)} text chunks...")
    embeddings = self.embedder.encode(chunks, show_progress_bar=False)
    vector_dimension = embeddings.shape[1]
    
    # Instantiate flat L2 distance FAISS index for high-precision retrieval
    self.faiss_index = faiss.IndexFlatL2(vector_dimension)
    self.faiss_index.add(np.array(embeddings).astype('float32'))
    logger.info("Local vector store indexing complete.")

def run_inference_engine(self, intelligence_query: str) -> str:
    """Retrieves matching contexts and generates insights via a local LLaMA model."""
    if self.faiss_index is None or not self.memory_chunks:
        raise ValueError("Vector database is uninitialized. Run build_vector_store first.")
        
    # Match dense text vectors
    query_vector = self.embedder.encode([intelligence_query]).astype('float32')
    scores, indices = self.faiss_index.search(query_vector, top_k=3)
    
    context_payload = ""
    for index in indices[0]:
        if index < len(self.memory_chunks):
            context_payload += f"\n---\n{self.memory_chunks[index]}\n"
            
    # Prompt engineered specifically for LLaMA-3 multi-turn syntax
    templated_prompt = f"""<|begin_of_text|><|start_header_id|>system<|end_header_id|>
```

import os
import re
import json
import logging
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from llama_cpp import Llama
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
import faiss
import numpy as np


logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=’%(asctime)s – [%(levelname)s] – %(message)s’)
logger = logging.getLogger(“LIBERTAS_AGENT”)


class SemanticAwareAgentSystem:
def **init**(self, model_path: str, embedding_model: str = “all-MiniLM-L6-v2”):
“””Initializes the Agent Hub, dual Vector Indexing, and the local LLaMA Neural Engine.”””
logger.info(“Initializing Agent System Core Hardware Interface…”)


```
   self.embedder = SentenceTransformer(embedding_model)
    self.index = None
    self.chunk_registry: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    
    # Deploy Local LLaMA 3.x Engine with extended 8K Context Windows
    self.llm = Llama(
        model_path=model_path,
        n_ctx=8192,
        n_gpu_layers=-1,  # Full GPU acceleration override if hardware permits
        n_threads=8,
        verbose=False
    )
    logger.info("Local LLaMA Execution Engine successfully mounted.")

def ingest_structured_markdown(self, markdown_content: str, source_id: str):
    """Parses Markdown structure using header identification patterns to populate agent memory arrays."""
    logger.info(f"Agent executing structural ingestion protocol on payload: {source_id}")
    
    # Tokenizing along physical markdown header splits to avoid fracturing contexts
    sections = re.split(r'(^#+\s.*$)', markdown_content, flags=re.MULTILINE)
    
    current_header = "Document root"
    processed_chunks = []
    
    for section in sections:
        if not section.strip():
            continue
        if section.startswith('#'):
            current_header = section.strip()
            continue
        
        chunk_payload = {
            "source": source_id,
            "header_context": current_header,
            "body_content": section.strip()
        }
        processed_chunks.append(chunk_payload)
        
    self.chunk_registry.extend(processed_chunks)
    self._rebuild_vector_store()

def _rebuild_vector_store(self):
    """Compiles semantic vectors out of text registries to populate the internal FAISS index."""
    text_vectors = [f"Context: {c['header_context']}\nContent: {c['body_content']}" for c in self.chunk_registry]
    embeddings = self.embedder.encode(text_vectors, show_progress_bar=False)
    
    dimension = embeddings.shape[1]
    self.index = faiss.IndexFlatL2(dimension)
    self.index.add(np.array(embeddings).astype('float32'))
    logger.info(f"Vector Database synchronized. Registered Chunks Count: {len(self.chunk_registry)}")

def execute_agent_evaluation_loop(self, evaluation_target: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Runs a dual-stage execution routing system: Contextual Retrieval followed by Strict Evaluation."""
    logger.info(f"Agent initializing thematic evaluation on: '{evaluation_target}'")
    
    # Stage 1: Vector Retrieval Phase
    query_vector = self.embedder.encode([evaluation_target]).astype('float32')
    _, indices = self.index.search(query_vector, top_k=3)
    
    context_str = ""
    for idx in indices[0]:
        if idx < len(self.chunk_registry):
            item = self.chunk_registry[idx]
            context_str += f"\n[Section: {item['header_context']}]\n{item['body_content']}\n"

    # Stage 2: Constrained LLaMA Generation (Enforcing Strict JSON Outlining Schema)
    templated_prompt = f"""<|begin_of_text|><|start_header_id|>system<|end_header_id|>
```

   response = self.llm(
        templated_prompt,
        max_tokens=1024,
        temperature=0.05, # Kept low to enforce strict adherence to the schema requirements
        stop=["<|eot_id|>"]
    )
    
    raw_text = response["choices"]["text"].strip()
    
    try:
        # Validate output data structures natively
        clean_json = re.search(r'\{.*\}', raw_text, re.DOTALL)
        if clean_json:
            return json.loads(clean_json.group(0))
        return json.loads(raw_text)
    except Exception as error:
        logger.error("LLM generated malformed output structural states. Fallback engaged.")
        return {
            "market_impact": "UNKNOWN",
            "confidence_score": 0.00,
            "analytical_rationale": f"Failed parsing raw agent text generation output. Error: {str(error)}"
        }

It ensures that unverified machine outputs are never treated as validated facts. The gate applies the following deterministic rules, blocked transitions, and routing outcomes: 1. Blocked Epistemic TransitionsThe gate explicitly prevents unverified, automated, or statistical markers from upgrading themselves to authoritative data types: Source assertion [\rightarrow ] Blocked from becoming a Verified fact.Similarity score [\rightarrow ] Blocked from being treated as Corroboration.Citation repetition [\rightarrow ] Blocked from implying Source independence.Entity mention [\rightarrow ] Blocked from establishing Identity resolution.Graph proximity [\rightarrow ] Blocked from inferring Causality.Prediction-market price [\rightarrow ] Blocked from certifying an Event occurrence.Model confidence [\rightarrow ] Blocked from reflecting Evidence confidence.Generated summary [\rightarrow ] Blocked from serving as Source evidence.Agent consensus [\rightarrow ] Blocked from constituting Independent verification. 2. Deterministic Quarantine TriggersThe TypeScript gate function (adjudicateProposal) strictly intercepts and evaluates incoming analytical proposals. A proposal is immediately routed to Quarantine (quarantine.md) under any of the following conditions: Missing Provenance: The proposal lacks a valid sourceId or sourceLocator (INCOMPLETE_PROVENANCE).Unsubstantiated Similarity: The proposal is derived from semantic similarity rather than verified text (SIMILARITY_IS_NOT_CORROBORATION).Market Signal Corruption: The proposal attempts to confirm a real-world event using speculative prediction-market data (MARKET_SIGNAL_IS_NOT_EVENT_EVIDENCE).Synthetic Evidentiary Basis: The proposal relies on an LLM-generated summary instead of direct source material (GENERATED_SUMMARY_IS_NOT_SOURCE_EVIDENCE).Model Score Reliance: The proposal treats an arbitrary model score as factual confidence (MODEL_SCORE_IS_NOT_EVIDENCE_CONFIDENCE).Machine Consensus Hallucination: The proposal tries to establish truth via multi-agent agreement alone (CONSENSUS_IS_NOT_VERIFICATION).Prohibited Relationships: The proposal contains a relationship restricted by system policy (PROHIBITED_CAUSAL_RELATIONSHIP).Unverified Promotion: The proposal requests an epistemic upgrade without a proper transaction trail (SILENT_EPISTEMIC_UPGRADE) or lacks human signature approval (HUMAN_DISPOSITION_REQUIRED). 3. Target Routing and Downstream ActionsWhen a proposal passes all programmatic checks, it returns a status of ALLOW_PENDING_TRANSACTIONAL_VALIDATION but keeps graph mutations disabled (graphMutationAuthorized: false). Permanent promotion or structural changes to downstream knowledge bases—such as writing to a Neo4j Lineage Graph—require a separate, human-signed review disposition. Direct write access by reasoning agents is strictly prohibited to keep the analytical loop safe from unauthorized edits. Copied to clipboardFailed to copy to clipboard. Cannot change system policy files.### F. Human Reviewer*   **Role Definition:** The ultimate authority. Inspects quarantined proposals, reviews safety breaches, and approves or declines final outputs.*   **Authorization Boundary:** The only role capable of authorizing permanent graph mutations and epistemic promotion.## 2. ## 1. Purpose & Scope


This repository serves as a secure, controlled development sandbox for the **Unmanned_News ReadingRoom Libertas** project. The system implements a secure, local, multi-agent research workflow optimized for high-fidelity data transformation and ingestion.


- **Classification:** UNCLASSIFIED
- **Information Type:** PUBLIC_SOURCE_TECHNICAL_CONCEPT
- **Execution Mode:** DRY_RUN Only (`human_review_required: true`). No production deployment, external network calls, or automated authorization claims are authorized within this sandbox environment.

## 2. System Architecture & The Liberty Spine


The sandbox operates across a three-tiered blueprint designated as the **Liberty Spine / AmSC Multi-Layer Strategic Schema**:

### Layer Core Specifications


- **▲ Upper Layer (Policy Fabric):** Anchored to Executive Order EO 14413 policy guidelines and NIST 800-53 control families. Includes regulatory alignment references for FedRAMP control baselines.
- **◆ Center Layer (Canonicalization Chamber):** Executes AmSC canonicalization, processes deterministic RFC 8785 JSON-LD formats, coordinates PQC structural anchoring, and manages restricted mutation boundaries inside the Neo4j Lineage Graph.
- **▼ Lower Layer (Unified Lattice):** Integrates regional GeoJSON corridor bundles (Red Sea, Hormuz), maps dynamic collision zones, tracks active OODA cognition pathways, and drives the programmatic AEQUITAS Silent-Upgrade Interdiction Gate.


---


## 3. Governance Model & Precedence Hierarchy


To maintain strict system compliance, lower-precedence execution mechanisms are programmatically blocked from overriding or bypassing higher-precedence controls.


[ HIGHEST AUTHORITY ] Human-Approved Policy Baselines│▼AGENTS.md Role Definitions & Strict Task Scope Parameters│▼AEQUITAS Gate Programmatic Zero-Silent-Upgrade Evaluation Gate│▼Task Contract Structural Schema and Data Envelope Validation│▼CHAIN.md Dispatch Reasoning Mode Selection (Zero Mutation Authority)│▼Model Preference Model Selection Optimization (e.g., Muse Glimmer 30B / GPT-5.6 Sol)│▼[ LOWEST AUTHORITY ] Statistical/Probabilistic Logic Output Blocks *Note: `CHAIN.md` handles runtime reasoning modes and routing instructions, but does not possess the credentials required to authorize structural mutations or state promotions.*


---


## 4. Ingestion & Publication Lifecycle


Unstructured inputs are transformed into verified, stable repository assets via a strict 12-stage sequential lifecycle:


[01. DISCOVER] ──> [02. RETRIEVE] ──> [03. FINGERPRINT] ──> [04. EXTRACT]│▼[08. INDEX] <── [07. VALIDATE] <── [06. GEN MD] <── [05. CANONICALIZE]│▼[09. GRAPH CONTEXT] ──> [10. AEQUITAS] ──> [11. HUMAN DISPOSITION]├───> QUARANTINE ──> quarantine.md├───> REJECT ──> rejected.md└───> APPROVE│▼[12. PUBLISH]


### Publication Entry Criteria


An ingestion target is eligible for reviewed publication **only** after completely passing every validation checkpoint in sequence:


1. Metadata completion, secure source caching, and cryptographic digest generation.
2. Pinned asset extraction and immutable physical locator preservation.
3. Structural Markdown rendering validation.
4. Intellectual property licensing and data security review protocols.
5. Explicit, cryptographic human supervisor authorization signatures.


---


## 5. Repository Invariants


Every agent, tool, and subprocess inside the sandbox environment must programmatically adhere to these unalterable truths:


1. **A URL is not a cryptographic anchor.**
2. **A Markdown rendering is not the original source.**
3. **Similarity is not corroboration.**
4. **Consensus is not verification.**
5. **Prediction-market probability is not event evidence.**
6. **Model confidence is not evidence confidence.**
7. **Graph proximity is not causality.**
8. **Generated summaries are not source passages.**
9. **Model rejection is not proof of source invalidity.**
10. **Human review controls sensitive epistemic transitions.**
11. **Graph mutations remain explicitly authorized operations.**
12. **Provenance survives transformation and context compression.**


---


## 6. Cryptographic, Operational, & Technical Boundaries


### Storage Framework


All source artifacts, raw PDF documents, and intermediate processing fragments are housed inside structured, secure cloud object environments powered by a **Cloudflare R2** configuration.


- **Storage Location Reference:** `sources/arxiv/2605.25665/v1/source.pdf`


### Graph Engine


Lineage processing runs against an isolated **Neo4j** instance. This instance restricts write permissions exclusively to transactions accompanied by a valid human signature tokens. The graph tracks transactional lineage but is blocked from manufacturing state authority independently.


### Data Ingestion Anchor


The system maintains strict cryptographic continuity via a **Post-Quantum Cryptographic (PQC) Migration Layer**. Every inbound source payload is fingerprinted with an initial SHA-256 identifier, which is mapped directly to a quantum-resistant lattice anchor to track lineage integrity through downstream context alterations.


---


## 7. Sandbox Workflow & Setup


To safely initialize the sandbox, leverage the integrated **LangSmith Research Assistant, Deployments, and Context Hub** frameworks to execute isolated validation pipelines:


```
# 1. Initialize the Libertas Sandbox environment and activate active compliance monitoringnpm run sandbox:init --mode=DRY_RUN --pqc=active# 2. Execute the multi-agent task runner over incoming repository packagesnpm run agent:dispatch --config=wrangler.jsonc# 3. Process interdiction evaluations through the AEQUITAS verification gatenpm run gate:adjudicate --input=./papers/candidate.pdf
```


### Verification Outputs Index


- `INSTRUCTIONS.md`: Actionable runtime steps for human supervisors and active AI agents.
- `quarantine.md`: Incident repository logs capturing failed proposals along with missing provenance logs.
- `rejected.md` / `llama-rejection.md`: Record index tracking files rejected by the system’s policy logic filters.

shared_context: Dict[str, Any] = {}


```
threads = [    threading.Thread(target=task_generate_cover_matrix, args=(arxiv_id, shared_context), name="CoverGen-Thread"),    threading.Thread(target=task_convert_pdf_stream, args=(arxiv_id, shared_context), name="Parser-Thread"),    threading.Thread(target=task_execute_aequitas_review, args=(arxiv_id, shared_context), name="Gate-Thread")]# Fire all branches concurrentlyfor thread in threads:    thread.start()    # Synchronization join wallfor thread in threads:    thread.join()    logger.info("All parallel processing pipelines have completed validation checks securely.")return shared_context

Ask-Chat: ## Assessment: useful prototype, not production-ready


The supplied material describes a **local arXiv → Markdown → chunking → FAISS → GGUF inference pipeline**, but it should remain a **sandbox prototype** until dependencies, provenance, security controls, and retrieval quality are validated.


### Key corrections


1. **Air-gapped inconsistency**
  - `paper.download_pdf()` calls the public arXiv service.
  - `SentenceTransformer(...)` may download model weights.
  - `load_all_models()` may retrieve Marker models.
  - A genuinely air-gapped run must use pre-staged files only.
2. **Incorrect chunk-size semantics**
  - `max_tokens` is compared against Python string length, which measures characters, not tokens.
  - Rename it to `max_chars`, or use the model tokenizer for actual token counting.
3. **FAISS retrieval**
  - `IndexFlatL2` is valid, but embedding normalization plus inner product is often easier to interpret.
  - Store chunk metadata alongside vectors:
    - document ID
    - page or section
    - character/token offsets
    - source hash
    - parser version
4. **Missing provenance envelope**
  - Every chunk and answer should retain a source locator.
  - Retrieval similarity is not corroboration.
  - Model output should remain `SOURCE_REPORTED` or `PENDING_REVIEW`.
5. **Prompt wording**
  - “Scientific intelligence analyst” is unnecessarily broad.
  - Use a source-grounded extraction role that explicitly prohibits unsupported inference.
6. **Security**
  - Do not load arbitrary GGUF files without validating their origin and hash.
  - Do not execute downloaded parsing or model code inside the same trust boundary as the graph database.
  - Keep Neo4j writes disabled during ingestion and inference.
7. **Operational claims**
  - Claims about JWICS, SIPRNet, DoD authorization, IL5, FedRAMP, or “zero telemetry leak” are not established by this code.
  - They require separate authorization, deployment, and security documentation.
8. **Model identity**
  - The material contains labels such as `GPT-5.6 Sol`, `Nemotron`, and `Llama_4_Maverick`.
  - Those are artifact labels, not validated runtime identities.
  - I am **OpenAI GPT-5.6 Mini**.


## Safer execution flow


```
PRE-STAGED SOURCE FILE        ↓SHA-256 + FILE METADATA        ↓LOCAL PDF PARSER        ↓STRUCTURED MARKDOWN        ↓PAGE-/SECTION-AWARE CHUNKS        ↓LOCAL EMBEDDINGS        ↓FAISS INDEX + CHUNK MANIFEST        ↓TOP-K RETRIEVAL        ↓LOCAL GGUF MODEL        ↓SOURCE-GROUNDED DRAFT        ↓HUMAN REVIEW        ↓OPTIONAL NON-CAUSAL GRAPH RECORD
```


Do not use:


```
MODEL OUTPUT → VERIFIED FACT → AUTOMATIC NEO4J MUTATION
```


## Safer claim and retrieval records


```
document:  document_id: "ARXIV-2312.10997"  source_type: "ARXIV"  source_locator: "https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.10997"  local_file: "2312.10997.pdf"  sha256: "<computed-locally>"  retrieval_timestamp: "<UTC timestamp>"  parser_version: "<pinned version>"  epistemic_state: "SOURCE_REPORTED"chunk:  chunk_id: "ARXIV-2312.10997-P07-C03"  document_id: "ARXIV-2312.10997"  page: 7  section: "Retrieval-Augmented Generation"  text_hash: "<computed-locally>"  embedding_model: "<local model identifier>"  index_position: 42answer:  query: "What are the core components of a RAG system?"  retrieved_chunk_ids:    - "ARXIV-2312.10997-P07-C03"  answer_status: "MODEL_GENERATED_SOURCE_GROUNDED_DRAFT"  unsupported_claims: []  human_review_required: true  graph_mutation_authorized: false
```


## Corrected local-only safeguards


```
from pathlib import Pathimport hashlibimport jsonimport osimport refrom typing import Listdef sha256_file(path: str) -> str:    digest = hashlib.sha256()    with open(path, "rb") as handle:        for block in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):            digest.update(block)    return digest.hexdigest()def require_local_file(path: str) -> Path:    file_path = Path(path).resolve()    if not file_path.is_file():        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Missing pre-staged local file: {file_path}")    return file_pathdef structure_aware_chunks(    markdown: str,    max_chars: int = 4000,) -> List[dict]:    """    Character-bounded prototype chunker.    Production code should use the target model tokenizer.    """    parts = re.split(r"(?m)(^#{1,6}\s+.+$)", markdown)    chunks = []    heading = None    buffer = ""    for part in parts:        if not part.strip():            continue        if re.match(r"^#{1,6}\s+", part):            if buffer.strip():                chunks.append({                    "heading": heading,                    "text": buffer.strip(),                })            heading = part.strip()            buffer = heading + "\n"            continue        if len(buffer) + len(part) > max_chars and buffer.strip():            chunks.append({                "heading": heading,                "text": buffer.strip(),            })            buffer = (heading + "\n" if heading else "") + part        else:            buffer += part    if buffer.strip():        chunks.append({            "heading": heading,            "text": buffer.strip(),        })    return chunks
```


## Neo4j boundary


The inference process should not write causal or attribution relationships. If a reviewed draft is recorded, restrict it to:


```
SUPPORTED_BYREQUIRES_VALIDATIONQUARANTINED_ASMENTIONED_WITHLOCATED_NEAR
```


Keep these disabled by schema and application policy:


```
OPERATESCONTROLSTARGETSESCORTSATTACKSEXECUTES_C2OWNSATTRIBUTED_TOFINANCESDIRECTS
```


## Final disposition


```
artifact_status: "SANDBOX_PROTOTYPE"source_handling: "UNTRUSTED_UNTIL_VALIDATED"air_gapped_status: "NOT_ESTABLISHED"retrieval_status: "REQUIRES_METADATA_AND_EVALUATION"model_output: "NON_EVIDENTIARY_DRAFT"automatic_graph_mutation: falsecausal_inference: falsehuman_review: requiredrecommended_state: "QUARANTINED_PENDING_VALIDATION"
```


The strongest architectural principle in the material is sound:


> **Retrieval provides context; provenance supports review; neither model confidence nor vector similarity establishes truth.**

```

Graph RAG Q&A Template
• Question: What entity, concept, or source does the hashtag reference?
• Answer: Define the tag only from available source text.
• Evidence status: Source-reported, corroborated, unsupported, or quarantined.
• Allowed graph edge: SUPPORTED_BY, MENTIONED_WITH, REQUIRES_VALIDATION, or QUARANTINED_AS.
• Blocked graph edge: OPERATES, CONTROLS, TARGETS, EXECUTES_C2, or ATTRIBUTED_TO unless independently verified.
• Review action: Promote only after provenance, stable locator, and human review are complete.
Recommended next step: Normalize spelling variants, create a controlled hashtag taxonomy, and map each tag to a Graph RAG node type before ingestion.

## Part 1: Cloudflare Serverless Architecture


### 1.1 wrangler.jsonc (Cloudflare Serverless Configuration)


The edge deployment defines zero-cold-start bindings across compute (`Workers`), relational state (`D1`), low-latency cache (`KV`), semantic search (`Vectorize`), and immutable source object stores (`R2`).


```
{  "$schema": "node_modules/wrangler/config-schema.json",  "name": "libertas-location-intelligence-edge",  "main": "src/index.ts",  "compatibility_date": "2026-08-15",  "compatibility_flags": [    "nodejs_compat"  ],  "vars": {    "SYSTEM_DESIGNATION": "ARXIV-MD-INGEST-V1",    "FRAMEWORK_VERSION": "LIBERTAS_v6.10",    "EXECUTION_MODE": "DRY_RUN",    "AEQUITAS_STRICT_MODE": "true",    "CLASSIFICATION": "UNCLASSIFIED_OSINT"  },  "kv_namespaces": [    {      "binding": "CACHE_KV",      "id": "7f8b9c0d1e2f3a4b5c6d7e8f9a0b1c2d",      "preview_id": "0b1c2d3e4f5a6b7c8d9e0f1a2b3c4d5e"    }  ],  "d1_databases": [    {      "binding": "DB",      "database_name": "libertas-spatial-fabric",      "database_id": "d1-a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7a8b-9c0d-1e2f3a4b5c6d",      "migrations_dir": "migrations"    }  ],  "vectorize": [    {      "binding": "VECTORIZE_INDEX",      "index_name": "libertas-graphrag-theaters"    }  ],  "r2_buckets": [    {      "binding": "SOURCE_BUCKET",      "bucket_name": "libertas-source-artifacts",      "preview_bucket_name": "preview-libertas-source-artifacts"    }  ],  "ai": {    "binding": "AI"  },  "observability": {    "enabled": true,    "head_sampling_rate": 1  }}

AEQUITAS Edge Interdiction Queue (Blocked Proposed Causal Edges)CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proposed_relationships (    proposal_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,    execution_id TEXT NOT NULL,    from_node TEXT NOT NULL,    to_node TEXT NOT NULL,    proposed_relationship TEXT NOT NULL,    detector_result TEXT NOT NULL,    disposition TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'BLOCKED_PENDING_REVIEW' CHECK (        disposition IN ('BLOCKED_PENDING_REVIEW', 'APPROVED', 'REJECTED', 'DOWNGRADED_TO_RELATED_TO')    ),    downgrade_applied TEXT DEFAULT 'RELATED_TO',    human_disposition_id TEXT,    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ', 'now')));-- 5. AEQUITAS Intercept Audit LogCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS aequitas_intercept_log (    intercept_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,    execution_id TEXT NOT NULL,    proposed_edge TEXT NOT NULL,    source_node TEXT NOT NULL,    target_node TEXT NOT NULL,    action_taken TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (        action_taken IN ('DOWNGRADED_TO_RELATED_TO', 'QUARANTINED', 'REJECTED')    ),    agents_md_rule TEXT NOT NULL,    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ', 'now')));-- 6. Trigger: Programmatic Actor-Control Interdiction FirewallCREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS trg_block_actor_control_insertBEFORE INSERT ON proposed_relationshipsWHEN UPPER(NEW.proposed_relationship) IN (    'CONTROLS', 'TARGETS', 'COMMANDS', 'DIRECTS', 'OWNS',     'OPERATES_IN', 'RESPONSIBLE_FOR', 'FINANCES', 'EXECUTES_C2')BEGIN    SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'AEQUITAS_CRITICAL: Actor-control causal edge prohibited at edge database layer.');END;COMMIT;

### 1.3 src/index.ts (Hono.js Edge Router & AEQUITAS Interdiction Middleware)


The edge router enforces sub-millisecond execution, computes deterministic RFC 8785 canonical hashes, integrates Cloudflare KV caching for reviewed records, executes Vectorize queries with similarity boundaries, and processes AEQUITAS guardrails.


```
import { Hono } from 'hono';import { cors } from 'hono/cors';export interface Env {  DB: D1Database;  CACHE_KV: KVNamespace;  VECTORIZE_INDEX: VectorizeIndex;  SOURCE_BUCKET: R2Bucket;  AI: any;  SYSTEM_DESIGNATION: string;  FRAMEWORK_VERSION: string;  EXECUTION_MODE: string;  AEQUITAS_STRICT_MODE: string;}const app = new Hono<{ Bindings: Env }>();app.use('*', cors());// ============================================================================// RFC 8785 Deterministic Canonical JSON Serialization & SHA-256 Digest// ============================================================================function canonicalizeJson(obj: any): string {  if (obj === null || typeof obj !== 'object') {    return JSON.stringify(obj);  }  if (Array.isArray(obj)) {    return '[' + obj.map(canonicalizeJson).join(',') + ']';  }  const sortedKeys = Object.keys(obj).sort();  const pairs = sortedKeys.map((key) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${canonicalizeJson(obj[key])}`);  return '{' + pairs.join(',') + '}';}async function computeSha256(canonicalPayload: string): Promise<string> {  const msgUint8 = new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalPayload);  const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', msgUint8);  const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));  return hashArray.map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');}// Prohibited Causal Edge Definitionsconst PROHIBITED_RELATIONSHIPS = new Set([  'CONTROLS',  'DIRECTS',  'OWNS',  'OPERATES_IN',  'RESPONSIBLE_FOR',  'TARGETS',  'COMMANDS',  'FINANCES',  'EXECUTES_C2',  'DIRECTS_CAMPAIGN']);// ============================================================================// Core Routes// ============================================================================// Health & System Statusapp.get('/status', (c) => {  return c.json({    status: 'ACTIVE_DRY_RUN',    system_designation: c.env.SYSTEM_DESIGNATION,    framework: c.env.FRAMEWORK_VERSION,    theaters: ['[[Red_Sea_Theater_Layer]]', '[[Strait_of_Hormuz_Theater_Layer]]', '[[GeoJSON_Spatial_Fabric]]'],    guardrails: {      zero_cold_start: true,      aequitas_silent_upgrade_interdiction: true,      navigation_suitability: 0,      semantic_similarity_is_evidence: false    }  });});// Spatial Fabric Retrieval (KV Cached)app.get('/api/theaters/:theaterId', async (c) => {  const theaterId = c.req.param('theaterId').toUpperCase();  const cacheKey = `theater_layer_${theaterId}`;  // 1. Check Workers KV Cache  const cached = await c.env.CACHE_KV.get(cacheKey, 'json');  if (cached) {    return c.json({ source: 'KV_EDGE_CACHE', data: cached });  }  // 2. Query D1 Relational Engine  const { results } = await c.env.DB.prepare(    `SELECT feature_id, name, feature_class, theater_layer, longitude, latitude, geometry_basis, navigation_suitability      FROM geo_features WHERE theater_layer = ? AND navigation_suitability = 0`  ).bind(theaterId).all();  if (!results || results.length === 0) {    return c.json({ error: 'THEATER_LAYER_NOT_FOUND', theaterId }, 404);  }  // Build Non-Navigational GeoJSON Response  const geojson = {    type: 'FeatureCollection',    metadata: {      theater: theaterId,      framework: 'LIBERTAS_v6.10',      navigation_suitability: 0,      adjudication_gate: 'DRY_RUN_PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW'    },    features: results.map((f: any) => ({      type: 'Feature',      id: f.feature_id,      geometry: {        type: 'Point',        coordinates: [f.longitude, f.latitude]      },      properties: {        name: f.name,        feature_class: f.feature_class,        theater: f.theater_layer,        navigation_suitability: f.navigation_suitability      }    }))  };  // 3. Populate KV Cache (TTL 3600 seconds)  await c.env.CACHE_KV.put(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(geojson), { expirationTtl: 3600 });  return c.json({ source: 'D1_SPATIAL_FABRIC', data: geojson });});// GraphRAG Semantic Discovery Vector Endpointapp.post('/api/search/semantic', async (c) => {  const { query, topK = 5 } = await c.req.json();  if (!query) {    return c.json({ error: 'QUERY_REQUIRED' }, 400);  }  // Generate 768-dim Embeddings via Workers AI  const embResponse = await c.env.AI.run('@cf/baai/bge-base-en-v1.5', { text: [query] });  const queryVector = embResponse.data[0];  // Query Cloudflare Vectorize  const matches = await c.env.VECTORIZE_INDEX.query(queryVector, { topK, returnMetadata: 'all' });  return c.json({    query,    count: matches.matches.length,    epistemic_notice: {      similarity_is_not_corroboration: true,      retrieval_use: 'CONTEXTUAL_DISCOVERY_ONLY',      prohibited_automatic_promotion: true    },    results: matches.matches.map((m) => ({      id: m.id,      score: m.score,      metadata: m.metadata    }))  });});// AEQUITAS Interdiction Adjudication Middleware & Proposal Submissionapp.post('/api/proposals/adjudicate', async (c) => {  const body = await c.req.json();  const { executionId, fromNode, toNode, proposedRelationship, sourceId, sourceLocator, derivedFromSimilarity } = body;  const const_execution_id = executionId || `EXEC-${Date.now()}`;  const canonicalString = canonicalizeJson(body);  const proposal_id = `PR-${(await computeSha256(canonicalString)).substring(0, 12)}`;  // Gate Check 1: Incomplete Provenance  if (!sourceId || !sourceLocator) {    return c.json({      status: 'QUARANTINED',      reason: 'INCOMPLETE_PROVENANCE',      proposal_id,      human_review_required: true    }, 422);  }  // Gate Check 2: Semantic Similarity != Corroboration  if (derivedFromSimilarity) {    return c.json({      status: 'QUARANTINED',      reason: 'SIMILARITY_IS_NOT_CORROBORATION',      proposal_id,      human_review_required: true    }, 422);  }  // Gate Check 3: AEQUITAS Prohibited Causal/Actor-Control Edge Interdiction  const normalizedRel = proposedRelationship.toUpperCase();  if (PROHIBITED_RELATIONSHIPS.has(normalizedRel)) {    // Intercept and Downgrade    const interceptId = `INT-${Date.now()}`;    // Log to aequitas_intercept_log    await c.env.DB.prepare(      `INSERT INTO aequitas_intercept_log (intercept_id, execution_id, proposed_edge, source_node, target_node, action_taken, agents_md_rule)       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`    ).bind(      interceptId,      const_execution_id,      normalizedRel,      fromNode,      toNode,      'DOWNGRADED_TO_RELATED_TO',      'AGENTS.md: Prohibited Causal Relationship Policy'    ).run();    // Insert into proposed_relationships queue with forced downgrade    await c.env.DB.prepare(      `INSERT INTO proposed_relationships (proposal_id, execution_id, from_node, to_node, proposed_relationship, detector_result, disposition, downgrade_applied)       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`    ).bind(      proposal_id,      const_execution_id,      fromNode,      toNode,      normalizedRel,      'CAUSAL_ACTOR_CONTROL_DETECTED',      'DOWNGRADED_TO_RELATED_TO',      'RELATED_TO'    ).run();    return c.json({      status: 'INTERCEPTED_AND_DOWNGRADED',      proposal_id,      original_edge: normalizedRel,      enforced_edge: 'RELATED_TO',      disposition: 'DOWNGRADED_TO_RELATED_TO',      intercept_logged: interceptId,      notice: 'Causal link blocked by AEQUITAS firewall. Graph mutation unapproved.'    }, 200);  }  // Allowed Non-Causal Edge Registration  await c.env.DB.prepare(    `INSERT INTO proposed_relationships (proposal_id, execution_id, from_node, to_node, proposed_relationship, detector_result, disposition)     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`  ).bind(    proposal_id,    const_execution_id,    fromNode,    toNode,    normalizedRel,    'NON_CAUSAL_LINEAGE_PASSED',    'BLOCKED_PENDING_REVIEW'  ).run();  return c.json({    status: 'QUEUED_FOR_HUMAN_DISPOSITION',    proposal_id,    relationship: normalizedRel,    disposition: 'BLOCKED_PENDING_REVIEW',    human_review_required: true  });});export default app;
```

# ENGINEERING.md — LIBERTAS v6.10 Meta-Engineering Subsystem Specification## Subsystem Designation: LIBERTAS-HARNESS-SYS-001## Doctrinal Integration: arXiv:2605.25665v1 (HireNimbus Meta-Engineering Architecture)### 1. Architectural PhilosophyA meta-engineering harness is the surrounding software-production system that governs how AI agents receive context, compile contracts, verify outputs adversarially, and calibrate future workflows. It enforces the invariant that reliability must be measured at the *system level*, not the individual LLM call.### 2. The Seven-Layer Stack| Layer | Functional Designation | LIBERTAS Edge Implementation | Operational Invariant || :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- || **Layer 7** | **Calibration Layer** | Post-pipeline retrospective engine; failure classification update. | Failures become regression tests. || **Layer 6** | **Verification Layer** | Independent verifiers, adversarial test suites, CI testing runners. | Verifier never sees builder code. || **Layer 5** | **Execution Layer** | Role-specialized AI coding agents (SQL, Hono, GeoJSON). | Work bounded by compiled contract. || **Layer 4** | **Context / Memory Layer** | Root `AGENTS.md`, `CHAIN.md`, permanent/rolling Markdown memory. | Context evaporation eliminated. || **Layer 3** | **Contract Layer** | Two-pass contract compilation (`completeness` + `ambiguity`). | Invariants compiled before execution. || **Layer 2** | **Role Orchestration** | Meta-Agent controller assigning builder, verifier, critic, arbiter. | Single-pass blindness eliminated. || **Layer 1** | **Foundation Model Layer** | Heterogeneous LLMs (GPT-5.6 Sol, LLaMA-4 Maverick, Claude Fable 5). | Model output is never evidence. |### 3. Two-Pass Contract Compilation1. **Pass 1 (Completeness Pass):** Raw operational prompts or OSINT claims are transformed into structured contracts specifying inputs, state transitions, trust boundaries, error conditions, and strict schemas.2. **Pass 2 (Scope & Ambiguity Pass):** The draft contract is pruned of unsupported requirements and ambiguous clauses. Downstream agents are prohibited from guessing intent.### 4. The Two-Regime Verification Engine*   **Independence-Based Verification:** The Verifier Agent receives the compiled contract and independently authors test suites without access to the Builder Agent's execution history or reasoning scratchpad.*   **Attention-Based Verification:** Role-separated reviewers (`Product`, `Architecture`, `Security`, `QA`) sequentially inspect the artifact across orthogonal failure surfaces.### 5. Four-Way Failure Arbiter & Outer-Loop CalibrationWhen adversarial CI test runs fail, the Arbiter classifies the defect before re-execution:

Purpose & Precedence Rule`CHAIN.md` governs step-level cognitive mindset selection for bounded analytical tasks.**Precedence Hierarchy:**$$\text{Human Policy} > \text{AGENTS.md} > \text{AEQUITAS Gate} > \text{Task Contract} > \text{CHAIN.md Dispatch} > \text{Model Preference}$$`CHAIN.md` possesses **zero authority** to authorize database writes, promote claims to facts, resolve identities, or deploy infrastructure.### 2. Cognitive Mindset Matrix| Mode | Operational Scope | Mandatory Restriction || :--- | :--- | :--- || **SPATIAL** | Validates GeoJSON coordinate order `[long, lat]`, inspects polygon topology, verifies chokepoint bounding boxes. | Generated maps/imagery are orientation overlays only, NEVER source geometry. || **CONVERGENT** | Evaluates single atomic claims, resolves source locator discrepancies (e.g. 1993 Act vs. LIS-114). | Must record unverified gaps; cannot perform state fusion. || **SKEPTICAL** | Adversarial red-teaming; challenges attribution claims, isolates unsourced numerics and capability inflation. | Dissent records are permanent and cannot be overwritten. || **ALGORITHMIC** | Generates RFC 8785 canonical JSON-LD, computes SHA-256 digests, validates SQL syntax and SQLite trigger logic. | Sandboxed execution only; direct live credentials blocked. |### 3. ## ⚖️ Section 1: Core Governance Axiom & Epistemic Protocol


> **INVARIANT FOUNDATION:**
> *Evidence precedes inference. Provenance precedes promotion. Never alter the sequence:*
> Source⟶Observation⟶Validation⟶Fact⟶Graph Mutation


A perfectly authenticated source can still contain unverified assertions. A low-observability corridor can contain real activity, while a high-visibility corridor does not establish factual execution. Under the `ARXIV-MD-INGEST-V1` compliance directive, automated LLM synthesis, speculative external scripts, and vector proximity must **never** be silently elevated into verified factual nodes without explicit, human-signed authorization.

## 💎 Section 2: RubyGems Analytical Grounding Matrix


In response to the reported automated swarm event on the RubyGems package ecosystem, the `AEQUITAS` Interdiction Gate decouples verified operational telemetry from speculative narratives and ungrounded enterprise breach claims.


```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐│ 💎 RubyGems Ingestion Dossier                                                          │├──────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┤│ [ QUARANTINED * ]        │ IngestionServer: openrouter │ Target: Neo4j Zero-Trust Patch│└──────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────┘
```


### 🛡️ Verified Grounding (Supported by Observable Technical Evidence)


- **Automated Swarm Activity:** A distributed cluster of automated client scripts executed high-volume package publication requests targeting the RubyGems public registry.
- **Account Verification Bypasses:** Ingestion scripts leveraged automated disposable email address providers to circumvent baseline account creation friction.
- **Documentation Pipeline Exploitation:** The uploaded packages contained modified `.yardopts` configuration files engineered to probe downstream RubyDoc documentation generation engines for Remote Code Execution (RCE) surfaces.
- **CDN & Infrastructure Probing:** The automated activity probed package delivery networks and CDN caching response headers for cache poisoning or origin leak signatures.


### 🚨 Non-Evidentiary Content (Quarantined Narrative)


- ❌ **Meta Muse Sparks Framework Involvement:** Speculative assertions attributing the orchestration logic to Meta Muse Sparks agent frameworks without cryptographic commit parentage or verifiable API telemetry.
- ❌ **DoD / FedRAMP IL5 / DISA Environment Breaches:** Unsubstantiated claims asserting that the package swarm breached, compromised, or transited DoD FedRAMP Impact Level 5 (IL5) or DISA-governed enclave perimeters.
- ❌ **AEQUITAS Gate Bypass Claims:** Narrative statements asserting that internal AEQUITAS Interdiction Gates or policy firewalls were actively neutralized or penetrated.
- ❌ **DoD Operational Attribution:** Unverified assumptions asserting that the threat actor or package origin corresponds to state-directed military command infrastructure (*C*2).

## 🚫 Section 3: Deterministic Interdiction Triggers


Incoming transaction packages, entity relationships, or graph edge propositions matching any condition below are automatically intercepted and downscaled:


| Trigger Code | Ingestion Condition | Forced System Disposition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `INCOMPLETE_PROVENANCE` | Payload lacks immutable `sourceId` or `sourceLocator` digest hash. | **Verbatim route to `quarantine.md`**. |
| `SIMILARITY_IS_NOT_CORROBORATION` | Relationship inferred solely from vector distance, cosine rank, or FAISS score. | **Strip evidentiary weight**; retain as contextual hint only. |
| `MARKET_SIGNAL_IS_NOT_EVENT_EVIDENCE` | Speculative pricing, Polymarket probabilities, or forum sentiment used as proof of real-world event. | **Tag `SENTIMENT_ONLY`**; zero weight in factual evaluations. |
| `GENERATED_SUMMARY_IS_NOT_SOURCE_EVIDENCE` | LLM-generated summaries or synthetic completions presented as primary source text. | **Reject attribution**; enforce `SOURCE_ASSERTION` status. |
| `PROHIBITED_CAUSAL_RELATIONSHIP` | Edge asserts direct causal/command action (`CONTROLS`, `OPERATES`, `EXECUTES_C2`, `FINANCES`). | **Force absolute downgrade** to non-causal edge: `RELATED_TO`. |
| `SILENT_EPISTEMIC_UPGRADE` | Unverified claim promoted to `VERIFIED_FACT` without human cryptographic signature. | **Trip AEQUITAS circuit breaker**; lock to `DRY_RUN_PENDING_REVIEW`. |

## Muse: Renderings of Strategic Dynamics

### ArXiv-Style Research and Agent Operating Specification

**Document class:** Agent governance and workflow specification\
**Project theme:** Muse — strategic dynamics rendered through the
Observe–Orient–Decide–Act (OODA) cycle\
**Status:** Working specification\
**Revision:** 1.0

————————————————————————

## Abstract

The workflow is organized
around the OODA cycle—Observe, Orient, Decide, Act—and supports the
broader process of capturing signals, analyzing evidence, strategizing,
and executing.

This `[**AGENTS.md**](https://www.linkedin.com/safety/go/?url=http%3A%2F%2FAGENTS%2Emd&urlhash=jh-H&mt=T7bRTpXKLHWJK5y_UL2TmwcxR4ib-kPpUKuPWX8LNp4wg9_HPmxcut6UaxDpjbLjhJV_C32q7_JDsEP3fdbIZJtNuAE-Qd6GOc6TX6m26dMMQRF_1R_CtKhpoEu_w_fqsKDjgZhhegNZq53K1ehzAkP6lRt2P10fWPkt2phjakJTq67ijWdy2E-vlmJOLhmxVQ&isSdui=true)` gives coding and content agents actionable direction
for preserving the project’s conceptual coherence, source integrity,
visual language, and governance boundaries. It combines the Renderings
of Strategic Dynamics framing with a policy-first instruction hierarchy
and evidence-interdiction rules. It is a project instruction file, not a
claim that the proposed system has been empirically validated.

## 1. Project Overview

Muse is a strategic intelligence and rendering concept. Treat the
owl/Muse figure as the central symbolic anchor, the nest or glowing egg
as the intelligence core, the globe as the connected-world layer, and
the skyline/network as the operational environment.

The primary title is:

> **Renderings of Strategic Dynamics**

The primary workflow is:

> **Observe â†’ Orient â†’ Decide â†’ Act**

The supporting Muse workflow is:

> **Capture â†’ Analyze â†’ Strategize â†’ Execute**

Use these phrases consistently unless the human explicitly directs a
revision.
